Top Stories

 

Trump's State of the Union touches on retirement, tariffs <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgsyCigWgeBWcNEgRb?format=multipart>

President Donald Trump delivered his State of the Union speech on
Tuesday, touching on topics such as tariffs, taxes and the economy. One
key proposal would involve providing access to tax-advantaged plans for
private-sector workers who don't have access to employer-sponsored
retirement savings plans.
*Full Story: *401(k) Specialist <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsyCigWgeBWcNEgRb?format=multipart>(2/24), Bloomberg
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgszCigWgeBWcNERcu?
format=multipart>(2/25), Politico <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsACigWgeBWcNGVKv?format=multipart>(2/24)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgtvCigWgeBWcNVoCV>

 

 

 
 

Investment News

 

Trump's global tariffs take effect <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsBCigWgeBWcNKuRi?format=multipart>

President Donald Trump's 10% global tariff officially took effect on
Tuesday, following the Supreme Court's decision to strike down duties
imposed under an emergency-powers law. A Bloomberg Economics analysis
finds that the average effective US tariff rate will settle at about
10.2%. However, the administration is working on a formal order to raise
the rate to 15%, according to an official.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsBCigWgeBWcNKuRi?format=multipart>(2/24), Financial Times
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgsCCigWgeBWcNPozJ?
format=multipart>(2/24)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgtwCigWgeBWcNXtkW>

 

Fed's Goolsbee urges caution on rate cuts <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgsDCigWgeBWcNVCFy?format=multipart>

Chicago Federal Reserve President Austan Goolsbee said rate cuts are not
appropriate until there is clearer evidence inflation is moving toward
the Fed's 2% target, warning that 3% inflation "is not good enough." He
cautioned against front-loading cuts, citing persistent pressures in
services and housing, as markets largely expect the central bank to
remain on hold until midyear.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsDCigWgeBWcNVCFy?format=multipart>(2/24), Bloomberg
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgsECigWgeBWcNdllB?
format=multipart>(2/24), Finimize <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsFCigWgeBWcNmoAm?format=multipart>(2/24)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgtxCigWgeBWcNaSrJ>

Policy & Regulation

 

Certain RMD rules delayed <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsMCigWgeBWcNSNWY?format=multipart>

The IRS is delaying certain updated rules for required minimum
distributions under SECURE 2.0. The announcement postpones "the deadline
for retirement plans and IRAs to comply with certain RMD regulation
provisions that have yet to be issued in final form," said Mark Iwry, a
former senior advisor to Treasury.
*Full Story: *ThinkAdvisor (free registration) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgsMCigWgeBWcNSNWY?
format=multipart>(2/24), PlanSponsor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsNCigWgeBWcNWndL?format=multipart>(2/24)

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Consistency is key for money talks with relatives <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgsOCigWgeBWcNbgMm?format=multipart>

Holding regular financial discussions with family members can help
everyone get on the same page, allowing for discussion about
investments, spending goals and other topics. Consistency is key, and it
helps to create an agenda and hold the talk in a neutral location.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsOCigWgeBWcNbgMm?format=multipart>(2/24)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgtzCigWgeBWcNmafZ>

 

Finding the right risk profile for your retirement portfolio <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgsPCigWgeBWcNhuSb?format=multipart>

Investors approaching retirement should reassess their portfolios to
ensure alignment with risk tolerance. Johnny Rosier of Goal Planning
Strategies suggests considering a shift toward more stable investments
without becoming too cautious. "Dialing down the risk doesn't mean
pulling absolutely everything out of more aggressive growth investments
and moving it all to safety," he writes.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uOAwDffJfaDHmgsPCigWgeBWcNhuSb?format=multipart>(2/24)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uOAwDffJfaDHmgtACigWgeBWcNtIMc>

Top Stories

 

Helping clients understand annuities, tax planning <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVvBDffJfaDHtwscCigWgeBWcNBrLd?format=multipart>

Demand grows for products offering protected growth and predictable
income, and this creates opportunities for financial professionals to
add value for clients through annuities, writes Alan Roman of TruChoice
Financial. Tax implications should be part of the conversation, with
Roman emphasizing the importance of clear explanations. "People worry
about longevity risk, market volatility and whether their savings will
support the life they've worked to build," he writes.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVvBDffJfaDHtwscCigWgeBWcNBrLd?format=multipart>(4/13)

Brokerage and Industry News

 

How baby boomers are set to reshape aging <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uVvBDffJfaDHtwsdCigWgeBWcNEQRQ?format=multipart>

With baby boomers reaching their 80s, many are rethinking issues related
to spending, desired locations to live and health. In some cases,
boomers are prioritizing the concept of "healthspans" over life spans,
writes Wall Street Journal columnist Clare Ansberry.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVvBDffJfaDHtwsdCigWgeBWcNEQRQ?format=multipart>(4/13)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVvBDffJfaDHtwsuCigWgeBWcNAlUE>

 

 

 
 

Investment News

 

US growth driven by productivity as labor force shrinks <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVvBDffJfaDHtwseCigWgeBWcNJKAr?format=multipart>

The US economy is experiencing a unique scenario where economic growth
continues despite stagnant job creation, due to increased productivity.
The Labor Department reports that output per hour in the nonfarm
business sector rose 2.1% in 2025, and growth has averaged 2.1% over the
past six years. Economists attribute this to automation, artificial
intelligence and a more stable workforce.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVvBDffJfaDHtwseCigWgeBWcNJKAr?format=multipart>(4/12)

Advanced Markets

 

Preserving wealth through value transmission <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uVvBDffJfaDHtwshCigWgeBWcNgKAU?format=multipart>

High-profile examples illustrate the challenge of sustaining
generational wealth without strong values and financial discipline.
Advisors are encouraged to help clients transmit values to their
children through storytelling and meaningful conversations about work
and problem-solving. "We serve our clients well by providing sound
advice on investments, legal documents and tax strategies," writes John
Knowlton of Credent Wealth Management. "We serve them best when we give
them tools to guide their families in the stewardship of family wealth."
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVvBDffJfaDHtwshCigWgeBWcNgKAU?format=multipart>(4/13)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVvBDffJfaDHtwsyCigWgeBWcNSelt>

IRS offers more info on tips deduction <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVvBDffJfaDHtwsiCigWgeBWcNriFT?format=multipart>

The IRS recently offered more information about the occupations that may
qualify for tax breaks on tips. The agency lists eight categories of
eligible occupations, including entertainment, hospitality and personal
services.

uVvBDffJfaDHtwsiCigWgeBWcNriFT?format=multipart>(4/13)

Retirement planning: 6 risks and ways to mitigate them <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVvBDffJfaDHtwsjCigWgeBWcNDaSw?format=multipart>

Effective retirement planning requires not only asset accumulation but
also risk management. Key challenges to address include longevity risk,
market volatility and inflation, writes Shawn Maloney of Retire Wise.
Annuities and other financial products may aid the planning process.

Most Gen Z adults rely on parents for support <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uVrGDffJfaDHsQuECigWgeBWcNWWIE?format=multipart>

The 2026 Wells Fargo Money Study shows that 64% of Gen Z adults aged 18
to 28 rely on their parents for financial support, straining the
finances of 56% of those parents. Financial experts recommend clear
agreements and regular check-ins to manage this support effectively.

affordability and other issues.

Investors to keep close watch on Q1 earnings season <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVrGDffJfaDHsQuGCigWgeBWcNfpxS?format=multipart>

First-quarter earnings season is beginning, creating a new test for the
market as investors continue to deal with the impact of the conflict in
Iran. "Earnings are the market's chance to shift the conversation away
from geopolitics and refocus on the fundamentals," said Bret Kenwell of
eToro.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVrGDffJfaDHsQuGCigWgeBWcNfpxS?format=multipart>(4/12)

IMF's Georgieva says organization to reduce growth forecast <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVrGDffJfaDHsQuHCigWgeBWcNlDDH?format=multipart>

It will likely take time for global price pressures linked to the war in
Iran to ease, even if the ceasefire lasts, according to International
Monetary Fund Managing Director Kristalina Georgieva. Georgieva said the
IMF would reduce its growth forecast as a result of the conflict.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/

Helping clients who have postponed life insurance purchases <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVrGDffJfaDHsQuKCigWgeBWcNMNDu?format=multipart>

Some potential clients have delayed life insurance purchases, and
serving clients in the 45-to-60 age range requires education and
empathy, according to experts. Embracing a coaching mindset and
highlighting insurance's potential as a proactive financial tool can help.

How to manage an inheritance responsibly <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVrGDffJfaDHsQuLCigWgeBWcNYFPX?format=multipart>

Receiving an inheritance can be overwhelming, but treating it as "extra
money" can lead to impulsive spending and financial instability.
Attorney Phillip Reed of Reed Law suggests taking some time before
making major decisions, understanding tax implications and consulting
with experts.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVrGDffJfaDHsQuLCigWgeBWcNYFPX?format=multipart>(4/12)

emergency expenses, covering monthly bills and paying off personal debt,

finances is shaping how and how fast they retire," according to Rita
Assaf, Fidelity vice president of retirement products.

Key inflation gauge remains elevated above 2% goal <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVlvDffJfaDHsGfLCigWgeBWcNRupy?format=multipart>

The personal consumption expenditures price index increased 2.8% from a
year ago in February, and a core measure that strips out the food and
energy categories gained 3%, according to the Commerce Department. Both
measures gained 0.4% on a monthly basis.

uVlvDffJfaDHsGfPCigWgeBWcNOqLx?format=multipart>

Gross domestic product growth for the fourth quarter of 2025 has been
revised downward to an annualized rate of 0.5% from an initial estimate
of 1.4%, according to the Commerce Department. The revision is primarily
attributed to lower investment, particularly in private inventories and
wholesale trade.

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Helping older relatives plan for LTC needs <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uVlvDffJfaDHsGfWCigWgeBWcNGmoo?format=multipart>

It's helpful to take a proactive approach to addressing long-term care
planning with parents and older relatives, and working with a
professional will lead to better results in the long run. Consider the
value of documents such as powers of attorney, and review the role that
independent living facilities, assisted living facilities and other
arrangements may play.
*Full Story: *The New York Times <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVlvDffJfaDHsGfWCigWgeBWcNGmoo?format=multipart>(4/9)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVlvDffJfaDHsGgmCigWgeBWcNTkoS>

 

Incorporating health care costs into a broader retirement plan <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVlvDffJfaDHsGfXCigWgeBWcNSeAR?format=multipart>

Lifetime retirement health care expenses for a 65-year-old couple could
exceed $400,000, according to the Employee Benefit Research Institute.
Incorporating an annual financial cushion to cover health costs into a
broader plan is one way to address this issue.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVlvDffJfaDHsGfXCigWgeBWcNSeAR?format=multipart>(4/9)

uVfODffJfaDHsypeCigWgeBWcNlMoY?format=multipart>

An authentic, genuine approach is key for pursuing an effective referral
program, writes Beverly Flaxington of The Collaborative. "You have to
know what you want, find words that are right for you, and proceed
accordingly."
*Full Story: *Advisor Perspectives <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVfODffJfaDHsypeCigWgeBWcNlMoY?format=multipart>(4/8)

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Retirement planning should focus on resilience <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVfODffJfaDHsypfCigWgeBWcNsauN?format=multipart>

Resilience should be a key focus of retirement planning, and a bucketing
strategy can help promote stability, writes Joe Harl of The Guardian
Group. Coordination is another key focus. "This is the gap I see most
often: People have accounts, products and documents," Harl writes. "But
they don't have coordination."
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVfODffJfaDHsypfCigWgeBWcNsauN?format=multipart>(4/8)

Study: Many heirs may quickly exhaust their inheritances <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVagDffJfaDHsotPCigWgeBWcNsZcV?format=multipart>

Forty-two percent of heirs "had their net worth fall back to, or below,
their pre-inheritance level when measured about 12 months later,"
according to research from Texas Tech University and the University of
Alabama. Compared with other kinds of financial windfalls, heirs seem to
have a higher propensity to spend money from this source, according to
Russell James of Texas Tech.

Investment News

 

Core capital goods orders rise faster than expected in Feb. <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVagDffJfaDHsotSCigWgeBWcNqFKj?format=multipart>

Core capital goods orders climbed 0.6% in February, according to the
Commerce Department. Economists in a Reuters poll had expected core
capital goods orders to rise 0.4%.
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVagDffJfaDHsotSCigWgeBWcNqFKj?format=multipart>(4/7)

The impact of a falling labor force participation rate <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVagDffJfaDHsotTCigWgeBWcNrpVC?format=multipart>

The labor force participation rate declined to 61.9%, reaching the
lowest level since the late 1970s, excluding the pandemic years.
Productivity growth has helped to offset the economic impact of the
decline, but "there's an open question as to how much more productivity
growth we'll get in the coming years," said Greg Daco, chief economist
at EY-Parthenon.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVagDffJfaDHsotTCigWgeBWcNrpVC?format=multipart>(4/7)

Fed's Williams foresees little change to expectations for underlying
inflation <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uVagDffJfaDHsotYCigWgeBWcNPKeJ?

Federal Reserve Bank of New York President John Williams said that "the
story hasn't changed very much" about underlying inflation despite the
potential impact of higher energy prices. Williams also said that he has
lowered his economic forecast for US growth slightly, and he now has
higher expectations for headline inflation.

Developing a successful referral strategy <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uVagDffJfaDHsouaCigWgeBWcNjlyt?format=multipart>

Working with centers of influence and attracting referrals is key for
building a business, but a strategic approach is necessary to ensure
success with this approach, writes Brian Shapiro of Forum Advisor
Alliance. "Most importantly, recognize that business development isn't a
one-size-fits-all effort," Shapiro writes. "That's why your referral and
COI efforts require individualized support, resources and approaches."
*Full Story: *Advisor Perspectives <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVagDffJfaDHsouaCigWgeBWcNjlyt?format=multipart>(4/7)

Retirees may face unexpected spring expenses <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uVagDffJfaDHsoubCigWgeBWcNvdKW?format=multipart>

Retirees may face unexpected expenses during springtime that can cause
budgetary challenges, including costs related to home maintenance, taxes
and pet care. Using a bucketing strategy that ensures sufficient
liquidity for short-term expenses can be a key planning step.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uVagDffJfaDHsoubCigWgeBWcNvdKW?format=multipart>(4/7)

Top Stories

 

Life insurance addresses modern financial challenges <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUwiDffJfaDHseATCigWgeBWcNdWqA?format=multipart>

Savers are dealing with heightened uncertainty even as they continue to
"recognize the value of protection, savings, and long-term planning,"
says Matt Berman, CEO of Foresters Financial. With that context, life
insurance is taking on a broader role in financial planning. In addition
to income protection, "permanent life insurance can support tax-
advantaged growth, supplemental retirement income, and effective estate
planning, including managing estate taxes and preserving generational
wealth," Berman says.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUwiDffJfaDHseATCigWgeBWcNdWqA?format=multipart>(4/6)

Why savers are flocking to donor-advised funds <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUwiDffJfaDHseAUCigWgeBWcNlEWD?format=multipart>

Adoption of donor-advised funds has increased, according to providers,
with some high earners seeking to adjust to tax rules for charitable
giving. These funds may also be appealing for people with highly
appreciated stock.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUwiDffJfaDHseAUCigWgeBWcNlEWD?format=multipart>(4/6)

Index shows slower growth in services sector <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uUwiDffJfaDHseAVCigWgeBWcNuIlo?format=multipart>

Growth in the services sector slowed in March, with an index maintained
by the Institute for Supply Management declining to 54, down from 56.1
in February. Meanwhile, a measure of prices paid for inputs rose to
70.7, reaching the highest mark since October 2022.
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUwiDffJfaDHseAVCigWgeBWcNuIlo?format=multipart>(4/6), Bloomberg
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUwiDffJfaDHseAWCigWgeBWcNFgqn?

SEC proposes budget reduction <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUwiDffJfaDHseBcCigWgeBWcNbhIa?format=multipart>

The Securities and Exchange Commission has proposed an 11% budget
reduction for the 2027 fiscal year, lowering total funding to $2.08
billion. Enforcement funding would be $634 million under the plan.

uUwiDffJfaDHseBcCigWgeBWcNbhIa?format=multipart>(4/6)

Treasury Dept. picks firms to run Trump Accounts <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUwiDffJfaDHseBdCigWgeBWcNeGON?format=multipart>

Bank of New York Mellon and Robinhood will play key roles in the
management of the initial Trump Accounts, according to the
administration. Robinhood will develop a white-label app for the
accounts, and the Treasury Department will retain control over the app.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUwiDffJfaDHseBdCigWgeBWcNeGON?format=multipart>(4/6), InvestmentNews

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

These assets might be better to hold outside a Roth IRA <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUwiDffJfaDHseBfCigWgeBWcNpODd?format=multipart>

Roth IRAs can be powerful tools for tax-free growth, but they aren't
necessarily the right home for every kind of asset, writes Jeff Judge of
Chesapeake Financial Planners. Consider your options before using these
accounts for highly speculative investments or assets you are likely to
need before age 59½, Judge writes.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUwiDffJfaDHseBfCigWgeBWcNpODd?format=multipart>(4/6)

attendees can save nearly $200 per night—but only for a limited time. If
you haven’t registered yet, now is the time to act. Secure your spot
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUwiDffJfaDHseBgCigWgeBWcNxxjg> and book

Put Financial Literacy on Lawmakers' Radar <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uUwiDffJfaDHseBhCigWgeBWcNGAxR?format=multipart>

April is National Financial Literacy Month, and a chance to reinforce
the role you play in helping Americans build stronger financial futures.
Holistic financial planning leads to better long-term outcomes, and your
voice helps ensure lawmakers understand that. Take a moment to urge your
lawmakers to recognize Financial Literacy Month and the impact of your
work, help us reach our next goal. <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUwiDffJfaDHseBhCigWgeBWcNGAxR>

Top Stories

 

The upper middle class is growing <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXcSCigWgeBWcNcmCt?format=multipart>

The size of the upper middle class is growing, with a report from the
American Enterprise Institute suggesting that 31% of Americans were part
of this group in 2024. Wage growth over time, particularly for those
with college degrees, is one reason for the trend. However, inflation
and other issues continue to pose challenges, even for those at the
higher rungs of the financial ladder.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXcSCigWgeBWcNcmCt?format=multipart>(4/5)

Brokerage and Industry News

 

How financial pros can aid families with Trump Accounts <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUrTDffJfaDHrXcTCigWgeBWcNiAIi?format=multipart>

The upcoming introduction of Trump Accounts in July provides a crucial
opportunity to help children get an early start on savings, and
financial professionals will have a key role to play, writes Finseca's
Jennifer Fox. "The decision of what to do with these funds when a child
reaches adulthood is not automatic," Fox writes. "Financial advice is
the difference between keeping money invested at age 18 versus cashing out."
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXcTCigWgeBWcNiAIi?format=multipart>(4/1)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUrTDffJfaDHrXdmCigWgeBWcNTTNf>

 

 

 
 

Investment News

 

Value stocks' strong year threatened by Iran war <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUrTDffJfaDHrXcUCigWgeBWcNqjol?format=multipart>

Value stocks have outperformed growth stocks this year, with the Russell
1000 Value Index up 2.4%, driven by companies such as SanDisk and
Moderna. However, the escalating conflict in Iran has started to impact
these gains, causing a 4.3% drop in the index since February. The S&P
500's energy sector has benefited from rising oil prices, while other
value stocks have faced declines amid economic uncertainty.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXcUCigWgeBWcNqjol?format=multipart>(4/5)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUrTDffJfaDHrXdnCigWgeBWcNUDYy>

 

US inflation expected to rise amid Iran conflict <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUrTDffJfaDHrXcVCigWgeBWcNzmCW?format=multipart>

US inflation data this week could reflect the initial impact of the war
in Iran, with some economists expecting a 1% increase in the consumer
price index.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXcVCigWgeBWcNzmCW?format=multipart>(4/5)

Advanced Markets

 

ICYMI: Setting up high-net-worth heirs for success <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUrTDffJfaDHrXdaCigWgeBWcNRPfe?format=multipart>

The transfer of large amounts of wealth during the coming decades has
brought familiar concerns into view, including how inheritances will
affect children's lives. Parents may use a number of strategies to
address the issue, including helping shape children's values and
establishing trusts. "While trusts are powerful tools, they are most
effective when part of a comprehensive financial plan," write Russ Alan
Prince, Paul Saganey and Homer Smith.
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXdaCigWgeBWcNRPfe?format=multipart>(4/3)

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Steps for establishing an emergency fund <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXdbCigWgeBWcNSzqx?format=multipart>

Building an emergency fund is crucial for financial stability, with 53%
of Americans unable to cover a $1,000 unexpected expense from savings,
according to Bankrate. Six steps can help build an emergency fund,
starting with a target and a simple plan, writes David Abraham.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXdbCigWgeBWcNSzqx?format=multipart>(4/5)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUrTDffJfaDHrXdqCigWgeBWcNfbvN>

 

Tax refunds grow this filing season <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXdcCigWgeBWcNUDYy?format=multipart>

The impact of the tax and spending legislation is becoming apparent for
many Americans during this tax filing season. The average tax refund has
risen to $3,521, according to the IRS, rising 11% from a year ago.
*Full Story: *The New York Times <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXdcCigWgeBWcNUDYy?format=multipart>(4/4)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUrTDffJfaDHrXdrCigWgeBWcNlpBC>

 

5 steps to financial independence after divorce <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUrTDffJfaDHrXddCigWgeBWcNYdfl?format=multipart>

Financial planner Stacy Francis offers a five-step guide to achieving
financial independence after divorce, emphasizing the importance of
understanding one's new financial landscape, building a realistic
budget, making informed housing decisions, protecting retirement savings
and building confidence in financial management.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUrTDffJfaDHrXddCigWgeBWcNYdfl?format=multipart>(4/5)

Top Stories

 

Managing the challenges of caregiving <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUmPDffJfaDHrPvhCigWgeBWcNNTdB?format=multipart>

A limited workforce is one challenge affecting long-term care services
in the US, and family caregivers often deal with stress and competing
priorities. Taking preventative measures and discussing key issues with
older relatives can lay the foundation for a more successful approach.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUmPDffJfaDHrPvhCigWgeBWcNNTdB?format=multipart>(4/2)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvBCigWgeBWcNIpLZ>

 

 

 
 

Investment News

 

IMF: US has limited room for interest-rate reductions <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPviCigWgeBWcNYriA?format=multipart>

The Federal Reserve may have little room for interest-rate cuts this
year, given that inflation appears to be on track to fall back toward 2%
by the first half of 2027, according to the International Monetary Fund.
The IMF's review of the US economy suggests one rate cut could be coming
by the end of 2026.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUmPDffJfaDHrPviCigWgeBWcNYriA?format=multipart>(4/2)

Policy & Regulation

 

Maine governor now supports higher top tax rate <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvpCigWgeBWcNwxzw?format=multipart>

Maine Governor Janet Mills has expressed support for raising taxes on
the state's highest earners, representing a shift from her previous
position. Lawmakers in the state have proposed a 2% surtax starting at
$1 million for single filers, a move that would increase the top tax
rate to 9.15%.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUmPDffJfaDHrPvpCigWgeBWcNwxzw?format=multipart>(4/2)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvDCigWgeBWcNTxAp>

 

What to know about Washington state's new tax on high earners <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvqCigWgeBWcNBrhX?format=multipart>

A new law in Washington state will impose a 9.9% tax on income in excess
of $1 million, with the first payments due in 2029. Debate over the tax
continues, but financial professionals can help clients prepare by
addressing issues related to residency and compensation structure.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUmPDffJfaDHrPvqCigWgeBWcNBrhX?format=multipart>(4/2)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvECigWgeBWcNbggs>

 

Fed's Logan on energy prices, monetary policy <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvrCigWgeBWcNHFnM?format=multipart>

Energy prices could remain elevated in the short term, with Dallas
Federal Reserve President Lorie Logan noting that US oil producers may
not boost output. Discussing monetary policy, Logan said that "policy is
positioned to adjust to the data as it's coming in, and we're prepared
to make adjustments to the policy path as appropriate."
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUmPDffJfaDHrPvrCigWgeBWcNHFnM?format=multipart>(4/2)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvFCigWgeBWcNkjvd>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Effective succession planning for business owners <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvsCigWgeBWcNPnTP?format=multipart>

Business owners should begin exit planning three to five years in
advance to ensure a smooth transition and the continued success of their
business, writes Lawrence Sprung of Mitlin Financial. Sprung emphasizes
the importance of objectively evaluating potential successors, including
family members, based on merit rather than familial ties. "You need a
solid exit plan, including a suitable tax strategy, regardless of who
buys your business," he writes.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUmPDffJfaDHrPvsCigWgeBWcNPnTP?format=multipart>(4/2)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvGCigWgeBWcNuHAc>

 

Planning ahead to manage sequence-of-returns risk <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvtCigWgeBWcNYriA?format=multipart>

Retirees face "sequence of returns" risk, where the timing of market
gains and losses can significantly affect the longevity of their
retirement savings. Proactive planning can make a big difference, and
having a good handle on retirement expenses and income sources is key.
Establishing an emergency fund is also critical, as it can help retirees
avoid the need to withdraw funds during a downturn.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUmPDffJfaDHrPvtCigWgeBWcNYriA?format=multipart>(4/2)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUmPDffJfaDHrPvHCigWgeBWcNGzMF>

Top Stories

 

Americans say comfortable retirement requires $1.46M <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFoCCigWgeBWcNEnSu?format=multipart>

Americans say they need $1.46 million to retire comfortably, a $200,000
increase from last year, according to a Northwestern Mutual study.
However, 48% of respondents fear outliving their savings, and that tally
reaches 55% among millennials.
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUgXDffJfaDHrFoCCigWgeBWcNEnSu?format=multipart>(4/1), USA Today
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFoDCigWgeBWcNEYdN?

Brokerage and Industry News

 

Understanding market-linked life insurance <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFoECigWgeBWcNHcLO?format=multipart>

Market-linked life insurance strategies are gaining popularity as
Americans seek protection from market volatility and long-term growth.
Index options are expected to expand and evolve over time. "This
represents an opportunity. A broader set of index choices means more
ways to customize a policy to match risk tolerance, growth expectations
and time horizon," writes Darrel Tedrow of Lincoln Financial Group.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUgXDffJfaDHrFoECigWgeBWcNHcLO?format=multipart>(4/1)

Annuities, life insurance integrated into holistic conversations
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFoFCigWgeBWcNKBSB?
format=multipart>

Financial professionals are increasingly integrating annuities and life
insurance into holistic planning discussions, experts say. The trend
comes at a time when the idea of creating a "retirement paycheck" is
gaining traction, according to Ben Rizzuto of Janus Henderson Investors.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUgXDffJfaDHrFoFCigWgeBWcNKBSB?format=multipart>(4/1)

Investment News

 

Fed's Musalem on economic risks, central bank policy <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFoGCigWgeBWcNPvBc?format=multipart>

The economy is facing increasing risks to employment and inflation, and
the central bank should remain ready to respond in either direction,
according to Federal Reserve Bank of St. Louis President Alberto
Musalem. "Policy is well positioned to address risks to both dual
mandate objectives," he said.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUgXDffJfaDHrFoGCigWgeBWcNPvBc?format=multipart>(4/1)

Best Practices

 

How to understand what truly matters to clients <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFoOCigWgeBWcNJhAV?format=multipart>

Deeply understanding clients' needs by asking open-ended questions and
practicing active, empathetic listening can help financial professionals
build stronger connections, writes Beverly Flaxington. "Most advisors
have great questions they have developed over the years, but listening
with empathy — truly and deeply listening — is a practiced art,"
Flaxington writes.
*Full Story: *Advisor Perspectives <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUgXDffJfaDHrFoOCigWgeBWcNJhAV?format=multipart>(4/1)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFpeCigWgeBWcNaDps>

 

How advisors can address policy risk in retirement planning <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFoPCigWgeBWcNJRMo?format=multipart>

Financial advisors should lead discussions about policy risk to help
clients prepare for potential changes in Social Security, Medicare and
taxation, writes Glen Franklin of Jackson National Life Distributors.
"By making policy awareness a regular part of retirement planning,
financial professionals can help clients build understanding and
flexibility that foster long-term confidence and successful outcomes, no
matter how policy evolves," Franklin writes.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUgXDffJfaDHrFoPCigWgeBWcNJRMo?format=multipart>(4/1)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFpfCigWgeBWcNgRvh>

Annuities can offer retirees stable income, longevity protection
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFoQCigWgeBWcNLWup?
format=multipart>

Retirees concerned about outliving their savings can consider annuities
as a solution to ensure a steady income for life, writes Ken Nuss of
AnnuityAdvantage. Nuss explains the benefits of fixed-indexed annuities
and lifetime income riders.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUgXDffJfaDHrFoQCigWgeBWcNLWup?format=multipart>(4/1)

Social Security as a tool to manage sequence-of-returns risk <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUgXDffJfaDHrFoRCigWgeBWcNPvBc?format=multipart>

Social Security can aid efforts to insulate portfolios against sequence-
of-returns risk, writes Ray Harris of Social Security Claiming Experts,
who discusses strategies to delay benefits.
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUgXDffJfaDHrFoRCigWgeBWcNPvBc?format=multipart>(4/1)

Gen Z redefines American Dream, relies on family support <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvuPCigWgeBWcNoeOG?format=multipart>

Members of Generation Z have their own take on the American Dream, with
69% saying that business ownership is a key component of it, according
to Wells Fargo's 2026 Money Study. However, many rely on parents for
financial support, in some cases straining family finances.

Major stock indexes surged on Tuesday on optimism that there could be a
path toward ending the war in Iran. The Dow Jones Industrial Average

composite jumped 3.8%.

Policy & Regulation

 

ICYMI: Washington state enacts tax on incomes above $1M <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvuXCigWgeBWcNrSEa?format=multipart>

Washington Gov. Bob Ferguson signed legislation to impose a 9.9% tax on
incomes in excess of $1 million, with the money earmarked for child care
programs, tax breaks for small businesses and other purposes.
*Full Story: *Washington State Standard (Olympia) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvuXCigWgeBWcNrSEa?
format=multipart>(3/30), The Wall Street Journal <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvvaCigWgeBWcNwxRY?format=multipart>(3/30)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvvrCigWgeBWcNYrLu>

 

FINRA portal to aid sharing of cybersecurity, fraud threats <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvvbCigWgeBWcNxidr?format=multipart>

A new portal launched by FINRA is designed to help broker-dealers share
information about cybersecurity and fraud threats. FINRA said it piloted
the portal, known as the Financial Intelligence Fusion Center, with a
"diverse group" of member firms last year.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUbrDffJfaDHrvvbCigWgeBWcNxidr?format=multipart>(3/31)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvvsCigWgeBWcNgarx>

 

Kansas City Fed's Schmid cautious about impact of energy prices on
inflation <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvvcCigWgeBWcNzmLs?
format=multipart>

Kansas City Federal Reserve President Jeff Schmid has warned against
assuming that energy prices' impact on inflation will be only temporary.
Schmid noted that inflation was already elevated even before the surge
in energy prices. "I don't think we can be complacent about the risks to
inflation expectations," Schmid said.
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUbrDffJfaDHrvvcCigWgeBWcNzmLs?format=multipart>(3/31)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvvtCigWgeBWcNpdGi>

 

 

 
 

Best Practices

 

Managing the emotional side of leadership transition <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvvdCigWgeBWcNCLSf?format=multipart>

Even people who have meticulously planned for succession at their
financial practice may have difficulty making the transition, writes
Scott Danner of Steward Partners. "Every founder must eventually
confront a more visceral question: Who am I without this firm? And
perhaps most importantly: What comes next?" Danner writes.
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUbrDffJfaDHrvvdCigWgeBWcNCLSf?format=multipart>(3/31)

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

What's the best way to save for education? <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvveCigWgeBWcNHFAG?format=multipart>

Some financial experts prefer to save for college outside of 529 savings
accounts, opting for flexibility over potential tax benefits. David
Blanchett of Prudential Financial embraces this approach, noting that
the "best thing we can do for our kids is to be very stable financially
and have lots of emergency savings."
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUbrDffJfaDHrvveCigWgeBWcNHFAG?format=multipart>(3/31)

Upcoming Experts in the Field Call: Marketing by Career Stage: What to
Do Now and What to Do Next <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUbrDffJfaDHrvvfCigWgeBWcNNTGv?format=multipart>

Marketing should evolve as your career evolves—what works in Year 1
won’t sustain you in Year 6. Join this call Thursday, April 2 at 12 PM
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvvfCigWgeBWcNNTGv> to learn

ICYMI: Monday Brief with Marc Cadin <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uUbrDffJfaDHrvvgCigWgeBWcNVCmy?format=multipart>

Last week, Finseca CEO Marc Cadin opened a Politico-hosted Economic
Forum alongside Members of Congress, Administration leaders, and
national press to discuss the U.S. economic outlook. He highlighted
growing concern around a potential “perfect storm” driven by rising
federal debt, challenges facing Social Security and Medicare, and
increasing workforce uncertainty as technology reshapes the economy—all
unfolding in a highly polarized environment where compromise is
increasingly difficult. Watch the full Monday Brief in your inbox or
here. <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uUbrDffJfaDHrvvgCigWgeBWcNVCmy>

Top Stories

 

Labor Dept. issuing proposal on alts in 401(k)s <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmaNCigWgeBWcNDiAr?format=multipart>

The Department of Labor is issuing a rule proposal focused on the
inclusion of alternative assets in 401(k) plans. "This proposed rule
will show how plans can consider products that better reflect the
investment landscape as it exists today," said Labor Secretary Lori
Chavez-DeRemer. Separately, Securities and Exchange Commission Chair
Paul Atkins said that "Americans' ability to participate more fully in
innovation and economic growth through well-diversified long-term
investments is a vitally important priority for effective retirement
planning."
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTxtDffJfaDHrmaNCigWgeBWcNDiAr?format=multipart>(3/30), WealthManagement
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmaOCigWgeBWcNIciS?

Brokerage and Industry News

 

Report: What investors expect for the future of taxes <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmaRCigWgeBWcNfcjv?format=multipart>

A Nationwide Retirement Institute survey indicates that 80% of investors
expect taxes to increase at some point in the future, but only 31% are
proactively modifying their financial plans. The survey also shows that
investors often view taxes as a once-a-year issue, with 34% paying
attention mostly during tax season.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTxtDffJfaDHrmaRCigWgeBWcNfcjv?format=multipart>(3/30)

Investment News

 

Powell: Fed watching inflation expectations <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmaSCigWgeBWcNpAou?format=multipart>

Federal Reserve Chair Jerome Powell said the central bank can look past
Iran's war energy shock for now but will keep tabs on inflation
expectations. At the moment, expectations appear to be "well anchored
beyond the short term," Powell said.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTxtDffJfaDHrmaSCigWgeBWcNpAou?format=multipart>(3/30), CNBC <https://

SEC semiannual reporting proposal moves to White House <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmaZCigWgeBWcNIciS?format=multipart>

A Securities and Exchange Commission proposal linked to semiannual
corporate disclosures has advanced to the White House for review. Last
year, President Donald Trump called for moving to a semiannual schedule
for reports rather than a quarterly one.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTxtDffJfaDHrmaZCigWgeBWcNIciS?format=multipart>(3/30)

Fed's Williams: Policy stance balanced to deal with range of challenges
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmbaCigWgeBWcNMVRt?
format=multipart>

New York Federal Reserve President John Williams says current monetary
policy is well-positioned to handle a wide range of issues. Williams
indicated that conflict in the Middle East could increase inflation in
the coming months but that the effect could ease later in the year.
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTxtDffJfaDHrmbaCigWgeBWcNMVRt?format=multipart>(3/30)

Best Practices

 

Managing generational transitions in family philanthropy <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmbbCigWgeBWcNTjXi?format=multipart>

Family philanthropy can face challenges during generational transitions,
particularly in balancing legacy with evolving needs. As boardroom
discussions often reflect a clash of values, structured dialogue and a
clear understanding of priorities are essential, writes Mike Wang of
Building Impact Partners. "Families must accept that philanthropy will
not magically erase deep-seated differences," Wang writes. "You can
share a common charitable vehicle without fully sharing a worldview."
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTxtDffJfaDHrmbbCigWgeBWcNTjXi?format=multipart>(3/30)

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Balancing retirement, care for children with disabilities <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmbcCigWgeBWcNaSDl?format=multipart>

Parents of children with disabilities face the challenge of funding
their own retirement while ensuring long-term care for their children.
Special needs trusts, life insurance and government assistance may play
into the financial strategy.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTxtDffJfaDHrmbcCigWgeBWcNaSDl?format=multipart>(3/30)

Finseca News

 

ICYMI: Finseca Flash: Department of Labor Promotes Lifetime Income
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmbdCigWgeBWcNjVRW?
format=multipart>

Through its new proposed rule, the Department is actively promoting
lifetime income by making it easier for retirement plans to offer tools
that can turn savings into guaranteed lifetime income. The proposal
would also create a clear regulatory safe harbor for ERISA fiduciary
investment decisions, giving plan sponsors and their advisors more
confidence to use these solutions. Read the full Finseca Flash here.
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTxtDffJfaDHrmbdCigWgeBWcNjVRW>

study, Inside the Intermediary 5.0: Executive Summary. Join NAILBA Chair

When is the right time for heirs to inherit? <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uTsFDffJfaDHrfwTCigWgeBWcNhRmd?format=multipart>

Planners are considering the timing of inheritance for heirs as
Americans live longer and wealth transfers occur later in life. While
receiving an inheritance at a younger age can be risky, inheriting later
in life may have less impact. Lifetime gifting and structured
distributions are methods to transfer wealth more effectively, planners say.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTsFDffJfaDHrfwTCigWgeBWcNhRmd?format=multipart>(3/29)

ICYMI: Unpaid caregiving exceeds $1T per year <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uTsFDffJfaDHrfwUCigWgeBWcNjVUe?format=multipart>

Unpaid family caregivers in the US provide over $1 trillion worth of
care annually, according to an AARP report. The report highlights the
financial and personal strain on caregivers, whose contributions exceed
Medicaid spending.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTsFDffJfaDHrfwUCigWgeBWcNjVUe?format=multipart>(3/27)

Investment News

 

Investors explore opportunity in emerging-market bonds <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTsFDffJfaDHrfwVCigWgeBWcNnvaR?format=multipart>

Some investors are buying emerging-market securities on the belief that
central banks will cut interest rates to prevent a growth shock. "The
market has priced the wrong risk," said Jean-Charles Sambor of TT
International Asset Management. "We've started to buy emerging-market
credit and local bonds."
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTsFDffJfaDHrfwVCigWgeBWcNnvaR?format=multipart>(3/29)

Fed officials signal caution about further interest-rate cuts <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTsFDffJfaDHrfwWCigWgeBWcNsoJs?format=multipart>

Federal Reserve policymakers are officially projecting interest-rate
cuts in the future, but there has been a more hawkish shift recently,
with Governor Lisa Cook pointing to concerns about higher energy prices.
Meanwhile, Chicago Fed President Austan Goolsbee said that "we could be
back to the era of multiple rate cuts for the year, if inflation
behaves." However, he noted that "I could see circumstances where we
would need to raise rates."
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTsFDffJfaDHrfwWCigWgeBWcNsoJs?format=multipart>(3/29)

How the Labor Dept. could increase access to in-plan annuities <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTlVDffJfaDHqUAECigWgeBWcNXtpQ?format=multipart>

The Employee Benefits Security Administration could increase the
availability of lifetime income options in 401(k)s by addressing
liquidity requirements and other issues. In a 2023 report, LIMRA noted
that around 9 in 10 defined contribution plans did not provide in-plan
options for lifetime guaranteed income.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg Law <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTlVDffJfaDHqUAECigWgeBWcNXtpQ?format=multipart>(3/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTlVDffJfaDHqUBaCigWgeBWcNqUNY>

 

Bill seeks to eliminate Social Security retirement earnings test
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTlVDffJfaDHqUAFCigWgeBWcNaSwD?
format=multipart>

A bill introduced this week would eliminate the Social Security
retirement earnings test, which may affect benefits for older workers.
However, workers may not realize that withheld benefits are paid back
over time after they reach full retirement age.
*Full Story: *ThinkAdvisor (free registration) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTlVDffJfaDHqUAFCigWgeBWcNaSwD?format=multipart>(3/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTlVDffJfaDHqUBbCigWgeBWcNrEZr>

 

 

 
 

Best Practices

 

What to know about serving dentists' financial needs <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTlVDffJfaDHqUAGCigWgeBWcNfMfe?format=multipart>

Dentists tend to have high incomes and may make appealing clients for
financial professionals, but there are also a number of complexities in
their financial lives. Dentists often have high debt loads from their
education, potentially affecting their investment and savings strategies
for years to come.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTlVDffJfaDHqUAGCigWgeBWcNfMfe?format=multipart>(3/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTlVDffJfaDHqUBcCigWgeBWcNtJHs>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Understanding the impact of the new deduction for older taxpayers
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTlVDffJfaDHqUAHCigWgeBWcNmakT?
format=multipart>

Retirees have an opportunity to save on taxes through the senior bonus
deduction, which is part of the 2025 tax legislation. The deduction
provides up to $6,000 per individual or $12,000 per couple annually.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTlVDffJfaDHqUAHCigWgeBWcNmakT?format=multipart>(3/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTlVDffJfaDHqUBdCigWgeBWcNxiOf>

Finseca's Cadin on the importance of access to financial planning
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLkkCigWgeBWcNHdFu?
format=multipart>

The US is facing a "perfect storm" of sorts, with the country grappling
with record federal debt as well as AI-related disruption and financial
challenges in the Social Security system, says Finseca CEO Marc Cadin.
"I think that ... we need to treat access to financial planning for all
Americans as a core part of the policy discussion, not an afterthought,"
Cadin says.
*Full Story: *Politico <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLkkCigWgeBWcNHdFu?format=multipart>(3/25)

Brokerage and Industry News

 

Frank Scalese honored as first recipient of Finseca's Robert "Bacci"
Baccigalupi Lifetime Luminary Award <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLklCigWgeBWcNKCMh?format=multipart>

Earlier this week Finseca announced that Frank Scalese, MassMutual
General Agent at Blue Oceans Wealth Solutions, was honored with
Finseca's Robert "Bacci" Baccigalupi Lifetime Luminary Award <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLklCigWgeBWcNKCMh> at the Leaders and
Managers Program (LAMP) 2026 in Nashville, TN, recognizing a lifetime of
leadership, service, and lasting contributions to the financial security
profession.

ICYMI: Analysis shows increase in high-net-worth households <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLkmCigWgeBWcNPwuI?format=multipart>

The group of Americans worth tens of millions or hundreds of millions of
dollars has expanded substantially in recent decades, with stock market
gains and other factors driving the increase. Around 430,000 US
households are now worth at least $30 million, according to a Zidar
analysis of Federal Reserve data.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLkmCigWgeBWcNPwuI?format=multipart>(3/24)

Investment News

 

US import prices see biggest increase since March 2022 <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLknCigWgeBWcNVKAx?format=multipart>

US import prices saw their largest increase in almost four years in
February, rising 1.3% amid surging energy costs. Core import prices rose
3% year over year, while imported capital goods saw their biggest price
increase in data stretching back to 1988.
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLknCigWgeBWcNVKAx?format=multipart>(3/25)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLlgCigWgeBWcNTrzL>

 

Number of ESG-focused funds declines in US <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLkoCigWgeBWcNdtgA?format=multipart>

The roster of funds focused on environmental, social and governance
factors in the US shrank last year, with 91 funds shutting down and nine
new ones launching, according to Morningstar. The development reflects
"ongoing consolidation and subdued product development in the
sustainable fund landscape," according to analysts Alyssa Stankiewicz
and Mahi Roy.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLkoCigWgeBWcNdtgA?format=multipart>(3/25)

Advanced Markets

 

Planning for an artist's legacy <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLkrCigWgeBWcNIMMN?format=multipart>

A situation involving the death of a photographer highlights the
unsatisfying results that may come about when someone passes away
intestate. Establishing a testamentary trust is one way to raise the
odds of a more favorable outcome, according to Marc Stern of Greenberg
Glusker in Los Angeles.
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLkrCigWgeBWcNIMMN?format=multipart>(3/25)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLliCigWgeBWcNbKoZ>

 

 

 
 

Best Practices

 

Don't wait to seek new clients <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLkuCigWgeBWcNQgYD?format=multipart>

Taking action early to win clients -- rather than waiting until
prospects age -- is crucial, according to research from Cerulli
Associates. Opportunities to engage with clients tend to decline over
time, and older prospects tend to have several preexisting financial
services relationships.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLkuCigWgeBWcNQgYD?format=multipart>(3/25)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLljCigWgeBWcNhYuO>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

How to navigate sibling dynamics when choosing an executor <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLkvCigWgeBWcNQRjW?format=multipart>

Selecting one sibling over others as an executor can lead to family
disputes. It's important to consider multiple factors when deciding who
should handle crucial decisions, including the complexity of the estate
and the difficulty of administering its assets, experts say.
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLkvCigWgeBWcNQRjW?format=multipart>(3/25)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLlkCigWgeBWcNpHaR>

 

When is the right time to take an RMD? <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTgmDffJfaDHqLkwCigWgeBWcNSVRX?format=multipart>

Waiting until late in the year to take a required minimum distribution
allows the money to remain invested for longer, while taking it earlier
may allow clients to cover expenses and mitigate anxiety about
downturns, write Robert Bloink and William Byrnes.
*Full Story: *ThinkAdvisor (free registration) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLkwCigWgeBWcNSVRX?format=multipart>(3/25)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTgmDffJfaDHqLllCigWgeBWcNyKpC>

Moira Buckley to serve as Finseca secretary, on track for presidential
term <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTapDffJfaDHqdmhCigWgeBWcNVoFH?
format=multipart>

Moira Buckley, Western Territory vice president at Prudential Advisors,
has been chosen to serve as Finseca secretary. The move puts Buckley on
a four-year leadership journey expected to culminate with a term as the
organization's president in 2028-29. "Serving in Finseca's leadership
team is an opportunity to give back and help move the profession forward
in ways that ultimately benefit the clients we all serve," Buckley said.
*Full Story: *Yahoo <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmhCigWgeBWcNVoFH?format=multipart>(3/24), InvestmentNews

Brokerage and Industry News

 

Savers may not know if they're on the right track <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTapDffJfaDHqdmjCigWgeBWcNrEXj?format=multipart>

In many cases, people may not have a good sense of whether they are on
track for a successful retirement, according to Michael Conrath of J.P.
Morgan Asset Management. "We've found that 56% of people don't know what
their target is, so if you ask them how much they should save or invest
and what number they're comfortable with, they can't answer," Conrath says.
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmjCigWgeBWcNrEXj?format=multipart>(3/24)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTapDffJfaDHqdmDCigWgeBWcNSzwL>

 

Financial fallout of gray divorce <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmmCigWgeBWcNtvcl?format=multipart>

Gray divorce is having a profound effect on retirement plans, with more
than 60% of Americans who divorce after age 50 reporting postponed
retirements and 67% reporting new debt, according to research from
Western & Southern Financial Group. Nearly 60% of respondents say they
have lost at least a quarter of retirement savings.
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmmCigWgeBWcNtvcl?format=multipart>(3/24)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTapDffJfaDHqdmECigWgeBWcNaicO>

 

 

 
 

Investment News

 

Economists: Fed rate hike remains unlikely <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uTapDffJfaDHqdmnCigWgeBWcNufnE?format=multipart>

Federal funds rate futures are pointing to growing likelihood that the
Federal Reserve could raise interest rates, but economists and analysts
say this is unlikely. In addition, none of the Fed's 19 policymakers are
projecting a rate hike this year.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmnCigWgeBWcNufnE?format=multipart>(3/24)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTapDffJfaDHqdmFCigWgeBWcNjlrz>

 

Index: Business activity at 11-month low <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmoCigWgeBWcNwjVF?format=multipart>

US business activity reached an 11-month low in March as war in the
Middle East put upward pressure on energy prices, according to an S&P
Global survey. The Composite PMI Output Index dropped to 51.4, with the
services sector declining and manufacturing improving.
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmoCigWgeBWcNwjVF?format=multipart>(3/24)

Policy & Regulation

 

Bill would expand IRA charitable giving options <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uTapDffJfaDHqdmsCigWgeBWcNSzwL?format=multipart>

A Senate bill would expand options for philanthropic donations from
individual retirement accounts by allowing qualified charitable
distributions to be directed to donor-advised funds. The bill,
introduced as a companion bill to House legislation, has been referred
to the Senate Finance Committee.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmsCigWgeBWcNSzwL?format=multipart>(3/24)

Best Practices

 

What to know about the QBI deduction <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmtCigWgeBWcNbCLw?format=multipart>

Careful planning is needed to make the most of the qualified business
income deduction for pass-through businesses. "Some deductions can
reduce QBI without helping eligibility," writes Matt Doran of &Partners.
"Others help eligibility without shrinking QBI."
*Full Story: *Advisor Perspectives <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uTapDffJfaDHqdmtCigWgeBWcNbCLw?format=multipart>(3/24)

James Martielli of Vanguard shares three strategies for teaching

principles.

Top Stories

 

Celebrating Excellence at LAMP 2026 <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTvvCigWgeBWcNeAts?format=multipart>

On Monday at LAMP 2026, Ed Deutschlander and Chris Noonan were inducted
into the Finseca Hall of Fame <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTvvCigWgeBWcNeAts> -- one of the profession's highest
honors, recognizing individuals who have demonstrated exceptional
leadership, advanced the industry, and made a lasting impact on the
financial security profession. We also extend our sincere
congratulations to all of this year's LAMP Leadership Award winners,
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTvyCigWgeBWcNlUFi> whose
leadership, dedication, and impact continue to elevate the profession.

Brokerage and Industry News

 

New life premiums hit fresh record <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTvzCigWgeBWcNmEQB?format=multipart>

New annualized individual life premium exceeded $17.5 billion in 2025,
reaching a fresh record, according to LIMRA. Total new premium was up
10% from the prior year.
*Full Story: *Reinsurance News <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTvzCigWgeBWcNmEQB?format=multipart>(3/23)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwvCigWgeBWcNtpgg>

 

Long-term investing focus key in the age of AI <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTvACigWgeBWcNoJyC?format=multipart>

BlackRock CEO Larry Fink's annual letter to investors highlights the
potential for artificial intelligence to drive significant economic
growth but warns it could also exacerbate wealth inequality. Fink
emphasizes the importance of long-term investing and broader market
participation to ensure more households benefit from economic expansion.
*Full Story: *The Guardian (London) <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTvACigWgeBWcNoJyC?format=multipart>(3/23), InvestmentNews
(tiered subscription model) <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTvBCigWgeBWcNsiFp?format=multipart>(3/23)

LIMRA: Annuity sales reach $464.1B in 2025 <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTvCCigWgeBWcNxcnQ?format=multipart>

Total annuity sales climbed to $464.1 billion in 2025, climbing 7% to a
fresh record, according to LIMRA. Meanwhile, fourth-quarter sales
reached $117.2 billion, up 14%.
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTvCCigWgeBWcNxcnQ?format=multipart>(3/23)

Goolsbee flags inflation risks <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTvDCigWgeBWcNDqtF?format=multipart>

Chicago Fed President Austan Goolsbee said he is more concerned about
inflation than unemployment as Middle East tensions cloud the policy
outlook. He said the conflict complicates the path to lower rates and
reinforced the need for clearer evidence that inflation is moving back
toward the Fed's 2% target.

uSwqDffJfaDHpTvDCigWgeBWcNDqtF?format=multipart>(3/23), Reuters

Average gaining more than 600 points as investors monitored updates

Best Practices

 

Helping high-net-worth women prepare for divorce <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwiCigWgeBWcNrUJu?format=multipart>

Financial preparedness is key for high-net-worth women undergoing
divorce. Michelle Smith of Savant Wealth Management advises women to
have a clear understanding of their living costs and discusses how fear
or grief can lead to suboptimal decisions.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews (tiered subscription model) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwiCigWgeBWcNrUJu?format=multipart>(3/23)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwACigWgeBWcNRJpn>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Trump accounts offer powerful retirement savings for children <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwjCigWgeBWcNsEUN?format=multipart>

Financial planners are urging parents to take advantage of Trump
accounts, which debut this summer and allow parents to get an early
start on tax-advantaged retirement savings for children at birth. One
strategy to consider involves contributing $5,000 annually for 18 years
and then converting the accounts to Roth IRAs, potentially creating
substantial tax-free retirement funds.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTwjCigWgeBWcNsEUN?format=multipart>(3/23)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwBCigWgeBWcNaMDY>

 

Prenups, real estate and financial security <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwkCigWgeBWcNuJCO?format=multipart>

Discussing a prenup can be awkward for clients, but it's also an
opportunity for forward-looking planning and clarity, experts say.
Prenups can be part of a broader conversation that also touches on
trusts, real estate and risk management. "A prenup is a financial love
letter to yourself," says real estate broker Alex Edwards.
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTwkCigWgeBWcNuJCO?format=multipart>(3/23)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwCCigWgeBWcNlkIX>

 

How annuities help ensure security in retirement <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwlCigWgeBWcNyiJB?format=multipart>

Current retirees are more likely than their peers who are still in the
workforce to have access to defined benefit pension plans. This
disparity could change the calculus for pre-retirees and emphasizes the
potential value of annuities to manage risk.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTwlCigWgeBWcNyiJB?format=multipart>(3/23)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSwqDffJfaDHpTwDCigWgeBWcNxcVA>

 

 

 
 

Finseca News

 

ICYMI: Monday Brief with Marc Cadin <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTwmCigWgeBWcNDcsc?format=multipart>

As financial planning continues to evolve, expanding access to holistic
guidance has never been more important. This week, Marc Cadin is joined
by Moira Buckley, Territory Vice President at Prudential Financial and
Finseca's incoming Secretary. With more than 30 years of experience,
Moira shares her perspective on advancing the profession through
leadership, service, and a commitment to giving back -- especially as
firms integrate wealth, protection, retirement, and long-term planning
to better serve American families. Watch the full Monday Brief in your
inbox or here. <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSwqDffJfaDHpTwmCigWgeBWcNDcsc>

Top Stories

 

Turning tax returns into planning opportunities <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNqYCigWgeBWcNhgeW?format=multipart>

Tax returns can be a valuable tool for strategic thinking about the
future. Financial planner Michael Pappis suggests reviewing whether the
standard deduction or itemized deductions were used, ensuring Roth
conversions are reported correctly, checking that retirement account
rollovers did not create unexpected taxes and adjusting tax withholding
as appropriate.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSrJDffJfaDHpNqYCigWgeBWcNhgeW?format=multipart>(3/22)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNrrCigWgeBWcNOpKD>

 

 

 
 

Brokerage and Industry News

 

ICYMI: Many Americans want to retire on their own terms <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNqZCigWgeBWcNqjtH?format=multipart>

More than 7 in 10 respondents to a Fidelity survey indicate that they
intend to retire on their own terms, with some planning to engage in gig
work, consulting jobs or entrepreneurship. "Retirement is being
reframed: It's no longer a single date and instead is an adaptable stage
in the next chapter," said Fidelity's Rita Assaf.
*Full Story: *ThinkAdvisor (free registration) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNqZCigWgeBWcNqjtH?format=multipart>(3/20)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNrsCigWgeBWcNQusE>

 

 

 
 

Investment News

 

Options markets revive 2022 strategies <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSrJDffJfaDHpNraCigWgeBWcNAHyG?format=multipart>

Options traders are drawing on patterns from 2022 as the Iran conflict
raises concerns about an inflation shock and sustained market volatility.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSrJDffJfaDHpNraCigWgeBWcNAHyG?format=multipart>(3/22)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNrtCigWgeBWcNTTzr>

 

Understanding gold's place in the investment landscape <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNrbCigWgeBWcNMzLj?format=multipart>

Gold is generally seen as a safe bet during times of inflation and
geopolitical uncertainty, but that hasn't necessarily been the case
recently, writes James Mackintosh of The Wall Street Journal. "The best
explanation is that gold is a crowded trade," Mackintosh writes. "As
with stocks, what went up the most in the months preceding the war fell
the most as investors pulled back."
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSrJDffJfaDHpNrbCigWgeBWcNMzLj?format=multipart>(3/22)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNruCigWgeBWcNYNhS>

Policy & Regulation

 

What Wash. state's income tax bill would mean for married couples
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNrhCigWgeBWcNQusE?
format=multipart>

Lawmakers in Washington state recently approved a 9.9% tax on income
exceeding $1 million per year. However, some observers note that the tax
includes a sizable "marriage penalty," because the same $1 million
threshold applies to individuals, married couples and domestic partners.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSrJDffJfaDHpNrhCigWgeBWcNQusE?format=multipart>(3/20)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNrvCigWgeBWcNfbnH>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Understanding the new tax deduction for older filers <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNriCigWgeBWcNVobf?format=multipart>

A $6,000 deduction for people 65 and older, starting in the 2025 tax
year, could temporarily reduce retirees' bills. Sam Tutko of Miser
Wealth Partners discusses how the deduction works and how to optimize
its value.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSrJDffJfaDHpNriCigWgeBWcNVobf?format=multipart>(3/20)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNrwCigWgeBWcNmJTK>

 

Retirement is an "endless game" for retirees to embrace <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNrjCigWgeBWcNbCgU?format=multipart>

Retirement should be viewed as an ongoing journey rather than a finish
line, writes Richard Himmer, drawing on the concept of "endless games."
Himmer argues that retirees should focus on continuous growth in
identity, purpose, learning and relationships, rather than simply
stopping work.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSrJDffJfaDHpNrjCigWgeBWcNbCgU?format=multipart>(3/22)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSrJDffJfaDHpNrxCigWgeBWcNvNiv>

Top Stories

 

Household net worth reaches new record <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSluDffJfaDHpCBhCigWgeBWcNYsjg?format=multipart>

US household wealth hit a fresh record in the fourth quarter, hitting
$184.1 trillion, driven in part by stock gains, according to the Federal
Reserve. The value of Americans' equity holdings gained $1.6 trillion.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSluDffJfaDHpCBhCigWgeBWcNYsjg?format=multipart>(3/19), InvestmentNews
(tiered subscription model) <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/

Investment News

 

Companies split large bond deals into smaller parts <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSluDffJfaDHpCBjCigWgeBWcNuIAI?format=multipart>

Companies are breaking bond offerings into multiple tranches to make
offerings more appealing to the market, according to London Stock
Exchange Group. The average investment-grade deal is now split into 3.3
tranches, representing an all-time high.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSluDffJfaDHpCBjCigWgeBWcNuIAI?format=multipart>(3/19)

Economic indicator index down in Jan. <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSluDffJfaDHpDaaCigWgeBWcNnCEk?format=multipart>

The Leading Economic Index from The Conference Board declined 0.1% in
January to a reading of 97.5, partly as a result of softening consumer
expectations.
*Full Story: *Seeking Alpha <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSluDffJfaDHpDaaCigWgeBWcNnCEk?format=multipart>(3/19)

Policy & Regulation

 

Labor Dept. returns to prior test after courts vacate fiduciary rule
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSluDffJfaDHpDafCigWgeBWcNEYsH?
format=multipart>

The Labor Department has removed the Biden-era fiduciary rule from the
Code of Federal Regulations after the rule was vacated in Texas federal
courts. The Employee Benefits Security Administration reverted to a
five-part test under the Employee Retirement Income Security Act.
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSluDffJfaDHpDafCigWgeBWcNEYsH?format=multipart>(3/19), 401(k)
Specialist <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSluDffJfaDHpDagCigWgeBWcNMGYK?format=multipart>(3/19)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSluDffJfaDHpDayCigWgeBWcNqrxE>

 

Bank regulators release proposal to soften capital rules <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSluDffJfaDHpDahCigWgeBWcNVKnv?format=multipart>

The Federal Reserve, the Office of the Comptroller of the Currency and
the Federal Deposit Insurance Corp. unveiled revised capital rules,
including the Basel III endgame rules, that would reduce Wall Street
banks' capital requirements by 4.8%. Banks with assets under $100
million would see their capital requirements decrease by 7.8% under the
proposal, while bigger regional banks would see a 5.2% decline.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSluDffJfaDHpDahCigWgeBWcNVKnv?format=multipart>(3/19), Reuters
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSluDffJfaDHpDaiCigWgeBWcNgisu?
format=multipart>(3/19)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSluDffJfaDHpDazCigWgeBWcNrbIX>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

What to know about the Roth employer match <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uSluDffJfaDHpDajCigWgeBWcNsaEX?format=multipart>

One provision in the SECURE 2.0 package allows employees to designate
employer matching contributions as Roth contributions. "While this
offers employees a valuable new option, it can also generate confusion,"
write Robert Bloink and William Byrnes. "The Roth contribution election
will trigger reporting and tax obligations -- and employees who made the
election may be receiving Forms 1099-R for the first time."
*Full Story: *ThinkAdvisor (free registration) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSluDffJfaDHpDajCigWgeBWcNsaEX?format=multipart>(3/19)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSluDffJfaDHpDaACigWgeBWcNtgqY>

Top Stories

 

Cadin: Vacating fiduciary rule aids retirement savers <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHpteNCigWgeBWcNGWkz?format=multipart>

This week, a federal judge in Texas vacated the Labor Department's
Retirement Security Rule, an attempt to expand the fiduciary definition
that was challenged in court. The "court ruling is a major win for every
American saving for retirement," said Finseca CEO Marc Cadin. "The
Fiduciary Rule placed barriers to Americans trying to save for
retirement. Removing this regulation makes it easier for retirement
savers to access the sound professional advice that creates results."
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSfLDffJfaDHpteNCigWgeBWcNGWkz?format=multipart>(3/18)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHptfKCigWgeBWcNaTjp>

 

 

 
 

Brokerage and Industry News

 

RILA sales continue to climb <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSfLDffJfaDHpteOCigWgeBWcNRupy?format=multipart>

Saltzman Associates' annuity market year in review shows that registered
index-linked annuities saw the highest growth rate last year, gaining
17%. "Frankly, I thought RILA sales would plateau at some point, but
they continue to grow," said Todd Giesing, author of the report. "This
product is now a teenager, marking its 16th year in the market, yet we
are still seeing more carriers entering the market and product sales
continuing to climb."
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSfLDffJfaDHpteOCigWgeBWcNRupy?format=multipart>(3/18)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHptfLCigWgeBWcNmLvS>

Producer price index up 0.7% in Feb. <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSfLDffJfaDHptePCigWgeBWcNdmCb?format=multipart>

Wholesale prices were up in February, with the producer price index
gaining 0.7%, largely driven by service costs, according to the Bureau
of Labor Statistics. A core PPI measure that strips out the food and
energy categories gained 0.5%.

Advanced Markets

 

Communication is key to effective estate planning <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHpteYCigWgeBWcNJLir?format=multipart>

Effective estate planning is about more than just taxes and legal
documents -- it requires clear communication to prevent family conflict
and ensure smooth business transitions. "Some of the most successful
transitions I have seen involved clients who took the additional step of
explaining not only what their plan does, but why it exists," writes
attorney Timothy Thurman.
*Full Story: *JD Supra <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSfLDffJfaDHpteYCigWgeBWcNJLir?format=multipart>(3/18)

Policy & Regulation

 

Fed holds rates steady; indicates one cut coming in 2026 <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHpteZCigWgeBWcNSOxc?format=multipart>

The Federal Reserve opted to hold its benchmark interest rate steady at
the end of its policy meeting, and the Fed's "dot plot" indicated that
one cut could be coming in 2026 and another in 2027.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSfLDffJfaDHpteZCigWgeBWcNSOxc?format=multipart>(3/18), The Associated
Press <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHptfaCigWgeBWcNdmCb?
format=multipart>(3/18)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHptfRCigWgeBWcNplGF>

 

ICYMI: SEC lays out digital securities framework <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHptfbCigWgeBWcNpeOE?format=multipart>

The Securities and Exchange Commission issued a "token taxonomy"
outlining which digital assets it considers securities. The guidance
labels stablecoins, digital collectives and digital commodities as non-
securities and specifies how federal securities laws are applicable to
protocol mining, staking and crypto airdrops. The guidance establishes
that digital securities are subject to SEC regulation. "We're not the
securities and everything commission anymore," Chair Paul Atkins said.
*Full Story: *CoinDesk (UK) <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSfLDffJfaDHptfbCigWgeBWcNpeOE?format=multipart>(3/17), Bloomberg
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHptfCCigWgeBWcNinvf?
format=multipart>(3/17)
