Best Practices

 

Helping clients make the most of a business exit <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHptfDCigWgeBWcNiXGy?format=multipart>

Many business exits do not yield the results that owners had hoped for,
so a proactive approach is necessary to make the most of these
transactions. "The exit wave is more than just a repeating cycle," write
Russ Alan Prince and Paul Saganey. "It is a fundamental shift that will
change how wealth is created, transferred and maintained in the coming
decade."
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uSfLDffJfaDHptfDCigWgeBWcNiXGy?format=multipart>(3/18)

What to consider before relocating in retirement <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHptfECigWgeBWcNlcoz?format=multipart>

Some clients may consider relocating in retirement to move closer to
family and manage their living costs and tax burden. However, "it's
important to look at the whole picture, financial and otherwise, as far
as relocating is concerned," writes Roger Wohlner, who discusses health
care costs, tax implications and considerations for those considering a
move abroad.
*Full Story: *ThinkAdvisor (free registration) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uSfLDffJfaDHptfECigWgeBWcNlcoz?format=multipart>(3/18)

Finseca CEO: End of fiduciary rule is a win for retirement savers
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRAWDffJfaDHpjlQCigWgeBWcNrTPS?
format=multipart>

Finseca released the following statement after today's federal court
ruling ending the Department of Labor's (DOL) Fiduciary Rule:
"Today's court ruling is a major win for every American saving for
retirement," said Finseca CEO Marc Cadin. "The Fiduciary Rule placed
barriers to Americans trying to save for retirement. Removing this
regulation makes it easier for retirement savers to access the sound
professional advice that creates results." Read the full comment here.

Wink: Individual life sales up in Q4 from prior year <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRAWDffJfaDHpjlRCigWgeBWcNvsWF?format=multipart>

Individual life insurance sales in the US reached $3.2 billion in the
last quarter of 2025, according to Wink, gaining 3.3% from the prior
year. The indexed, fixed and variable universal life segments made up
more than 57% of premiums from new sales.

Helping clients review irrevocable trusts <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uRAWDffJfaDHpjlVCigWgeBWcNXmFJ?format=multipart>

Older irrevocable trusts may have been established before recent changes
in tax policy, meaning that now is a good time to review these vehicles
and whether they are still serving their purpose. "Advisors increasingly
find that the question isn't whether a trust is irrevocable, but whether
it remains aligned with the family's needs, tax posture and
multigenerational goals," write Bronwyn Martin and Martin Shenkman.
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRAWDffJfaDHpjlVCigWgeBWcNXmFJ?format=multipart>(3/17)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRAWDffJfaDHpjmnCigWgeBWcNAWWh>

 

 

 
 

Policy & Regulation

 

Fed officials weigh impact of conflict on economy <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRAWDffJfaDHpjlWCigWgeBWcNhKKI?format=multipart>

The war in Iran is likely to weigh on Federal Reserve policymakers as
they meet this week, with the recent surge in oil prices having a key
impact on the economy. Analysts are expecting the Fed to hold interest
rates at the end of its meeting.
*Full Story: *The Associated Press <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRAWDffJfaDHpjlWCigWgeBWcNhKKI?format=multipart>(3/17), The Wall Street
Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRAWDffJfaDHpjlXCigWgeBWcNtCXl?
format=multipart>(3/17)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRAWDffJfaDHpjmoCigWgeBWcNDbEi>

 

Lawmaker looks to eliminate tax on home sales <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uRAWDffJfaDHpjmaCigWgeBWcNtYEZ?format=multipart>

Rep. Tim Burchett of Tennessee plans to introduce legislation to
eliminate capital gains taxes on property sales, arguing that the tax
discourages investment. The proposal would be a significant change from
current law, which allows individual homeowners to exclude up to
$250,000 in gains from the sale of a primary residence.
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRAWDffJfaDHpjmaCigWgeBWcNtYEZ?format=multipart>(3/17)

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Turning money fights into productive conversations <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRAWDffJfaDHpjmbCigWgeBWcNuIQs?format=multipart>

Financial planner Rick Kahler offers strategies for couples to transform
money fights into productive conversations. Kahler recommends shifting
from blame to curiosity, taking responsibility for one's emotions and
holding regular discussions. "Healthy money communication is ultimately
about connection," Kahler writes.
*Full Story: *Advisor Perspectives <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/

Top Stories

 

Cadin: Making the most of this year's large tax refunds <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbuaCigWgeBWcNoPJz?format=multipart>

Federal policy is expected to lead to large tax refunds this year, and
this money "can make a pivotal difference in creating an emergency fund,
preparing for retirement, and saving for college tuition," writes
Finseca CEO Marc Cadin. However, exactly what families choose to do with
the money will have a crucial impact. "Many families will consult
experts," Cadin notes. "In every community, financial professionals can
advise on how to create a portfolio that makes sense for a family and
their future."
*Full Story: *RealClearMarkets <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRvpDffJfaDHpbuaCigWgeBWcNoPJz?format=multipart>(3/16)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbuqCigWgeBWcNECKD>

 

 

 
 

Brokerage and Industry News

 

Life insurance planning opportunities during tax season <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbubCigWgeBWcNAHWc?format=multipart>

Tax season provides an impetus for proactive life insurance planning,
writes Carly Brooks of Crump Life Insurance Services, who discusses
potential insights from Form 1040. "Tax season doesn't have to be a
once-a-year administrative chore," Brooks writes. "With the right
mindset, it can become a valuable annual prospecting and planning
opportunity."
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRvpDffJfaDHpbubCigWgeBWcNAHWc?format=multipart>(3/16)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpburCigWgeBWcNFmVW>

 

 

 
 

Investment News

 

Stocks rise as investors consider oil prices, potential Fed moves
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbueCigWgeBWcNzJcc?
format=multipart>

Major stock indexes rose on Monday as investors considered the impact of
elevated oil prices and the potential path of the Federal Reserve's
monetary policy. The Dow Jones Industrial Average gained around 0.8% --
nearly 400 points -- and the S&P 500 and Nasdaq composite also climbed.
*Full Story: *Yahoo <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRvpDffJfaDHpbueCigWgeBWcNzJcc?format=multipart>(3/16)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbusCigWgeBWcNHrDX>

 

 

 
 

Policy & Regulation

 

Sources: SEC preparing proposal allowing less-frequent reporting
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbufCigWgeBWcNAtnv?
format=multipart>

The Securities and Exchange Commission is reportedly preparing to issue
a proposal that would allow public companies to report results twice a
year rather than on a quarterly basis. The proposal could come as soon
as April, sources say.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRvpDffJfaDHpbufCigWgeBWcNAtnv?format=multipart>(3/16)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbutCigWgeBWcNKQKK>

 

SEC's Peirce on updating the "small" advisory firm definition <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbugCigWgeBWcNCxVw?format=multipart>

A proposal to update the definition of a "small" advisory firm will give
the Securities and Exchange Commission a more accurate understanding of
the industry, according to SEC Commissioner Hester Peirce. "The
definition is so out of date," Peirce said.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews (tiered subscription model) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbugCigWgeBWcNCxVw?format=multipart>(3/16)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbuuCigWgeBWcNPKtl>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Navigating withdrawals during periods of market volatility <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbuhCigWgeBWcNFXcj?format=multipart>

Periods of volatility can create financial and emotional stress for
clients, particularly those who are dealing with market losses early in
retirement. It's possible to prepare by stress-testing portfolios ahead
of time and structuring holdings to avoid forced selling, writes Michael
Schessler of Sustainable Retirement Solutions.
*Full Story: *ThinkAdvisor (free registration) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbuhCigWgeBWcNFXcj?format=multipart>(3/16)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbuvCigWgeBWcNVYza>

 

 

 
 

Finseca News

 

ICYMI: Monday Brief with Marc Cadin <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRvpDffJfaDHpbuiCigWgeBWcNKQKK?format=multipart>

Engaging directly with policymakers helps ensure regulations support the
professionals serving American families. This week, Marc Cadin shares an
update on Finseca's recent meetings with leaders at the Department of
Labor, joined by ACLI President and CEO David Chavern, during which they
discussed key regulatory priorities and the future of holistic financial
planning. Watch the full Monday Brief in your inbox or here. <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRvpDffJfaDHpbuiCigWgeBWcNKQKK>

Top Stories

 

Understanding per stirpes vs. per capita in estate planning <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRrCDffJfaDHoTAhCigWgeBWcNBdkW?format=multipart>

When it comes to estate planning, the terms per stirpes and per capita
can significantly affect how assets are distributed to beneficiaries.
Per stirpes ensures that a deceased beneficiary's share goes to their
descendants, while per capita redistributes the share among surviving
beneficiaries.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRrCDffJfaDHoTAhCigWgeBWcNBdkW?format=multipart>(3/15)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRrCDffJfaDHoTAzCigWgeBWcNGVRF>

 

 

 
 

Brokerage and Industry News

 

ICYMI: What women should know about the Great Wealth Transfer <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRrCDffJfaDHoTAiCigWgeBWcNFWTx?format=multipart>

Women, who tend to have longer life expectancies, are expected to be the
primary recipients of spouse-to-spouse wealth transfers in the coming
years. Widowed spouses should prepare for changes in their tax
situations and shifts in cash flow, experts say.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRrCDffJfaDHoTAiCigWgeBWcNFWTx?format=multipart>(3/14)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRrCDffJfaDHoTACCigWgeBWcNLBfD>

How will the Fed respond to elevated oil prices? <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRrCDffJfaDHoTAjCigWgeBWcNMkZm?format=multipart>

The impact of disruption in the oil market is top of mind with Federal
Reserve officials set to meet this week. In addition to the impact on
inflation, observers are watching to see whether elevated oil prices
could eventually depress the economy.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRrCDffJfaDHoTAjCigWgeBWcNMkZm?format=multipart>(3/15)

States' income tax strategies vary across country <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRrCDffJfaDHoTArCigWgeBWcNDicl?format=multipart>

States are taking different approaches to income taxes, with some
seeking to lower rates and others looking to raise levies on top earners.

uRrCDffJfaDHoTArCigWgeBWcNDicl?format=multipart>(3/15)

NYC mayor suggests raising top estate tax rate, cutting exemption
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRrCDffJfaDHoTAsCigWgeBWcNFmKm?
format=multipart>

New York City Mayor Zohran Mamdani has floated the idea of raising the
top estate tax rate to 50% and reducing the exemption threshold by
almost 90%. The plan was part of a memo detailing potential revenue-
raising ideas that was circulated among state lawmakers.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRrCDffJfaDHoTAsCigWgeBWcNFmKm?format=multipart>(3/13)

Retirement "red zone" may be riskier than people think <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRrCDffJfaDHoTAtCigWgeBWcNILQZ?format=multipart>

People ages 55 to 65 might unknowingly take on more investment risk than
they are comfortable with during the retirement "red zone," the 10-year
period spanning five years before and after retirement, writes Andrew
Costa of Global Wealth Management.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRrCDffJfaDHoTAtCigWgeBWcNILQZ?format=multipart>(3/15)

Judge approves motion to vacate Biden-era fiduciary rule <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJowCigWgeBWcNyopm?format=multipart>

District Court Judge Jeremy Kernodle in Texas approved a motion to
vacate a Biden-era fiduciary rule, with the Department of Labor
unopposed to the motion. The Retirement Security Rule was stayed in July
2024, before it could take effect. The rule has faced multiple legal
challenges, including one involving Finseca.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg Law <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJowCigWgeBWcNyopm?format=multipart>(3/12), The National

Investment News

 

Major stock indexes sink on Thursday <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoyCigWgeBWcNRPIW?format=multipart>

The Dow Jones Industrial Average dropped nearly 740 points, and the S&P
500 and Nasdaq composite also sank on Thursday as investors continued to
weigh the impact of the war in Iran. The Dow closed under 47,000 for the
first time in 2026.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoyCigWgeBWcNRPIW?format=multipart>(3/12)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJoWCigWgeBWcNaiyk>

 

Schwab CEO: Gen Z investors are starting early <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJozCigWgeBWcNdHVz?format=multipart>

Gen Z investors are starting earlier and trading more actively than
previous generations, according to Charles Schwab CEO Rick Wurster. Gen
Z investors are 45% more likely to begin investing by age 21 when
compared with millennials. Members of Gen Z have been among the
investors buying the dip during recent bouts of volatility.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJozCigWgeBWcNdHVz?format=multipart>(3/12)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJoXCigWgeBWcNmaKN>

 

Trade deficit declines in Jan. <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoCCigWgeBWcNcJbz?format=multipart>

The trade deficit reached $54.5 billion in January, shrinking by 25.3%,
according to a Commerce Department report that was delayed by last
year's government shutdown. Imports were down 0.7% for the month, while
exports surged 5.5%.
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoCCigWgeBWcNcJbz?format=multipart>(3/12)

Policy & Regulation

 

Wash. state lawmakers approve tax on high earners <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJoFCigWgeBWcNiXbG?format=multipart>

Washington lawmakers have passed a 9.9% income tax for residents making
more than $1 million, with the state Senate approving the measure in a
27-21 vote. Gov. Bob Ferguson said he would sign the bill.
*Full Story: *KOMO-TV (Seattle) <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoFCigWgeBWcNiXbG?format=multipart>(3/12), Politico
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJoGCigWgeBWcNnQKh?
format=multipart>(3/12), Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoHCigWgeBWcNuePW?format=multipart>(3/12)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJpbCigWgeBWcNoBbi>

 

Regulators expected to ease lenders' capital requirements <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJoICigWgeBWcNBNvZ?format=multipart>

US regulators are planning to issue proposals that, when taken together,
could ease capital requirements affecting lenders, according to Fed Vice
Chair for Supervision Michelle Bowman. "These changes to the capital
framework eliminate overlapping requirements, right-size calibrations to
match actual risk, and comprehensively address long-standing gaps in our
prudential framework," Bowman said.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoICigWgeBWcNBNvZ?format=multipart>(3/12), The New York
Times <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJoJCigWgeBWcNKQKK?
format=multipart>(3/12)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJpcCigWgeBWcNqFJj>

 

Survey: Economists expect Fed rate cut in June <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJoKCigWgeBWcNVoPJ?format=multipart>

Economists anticipate the Federal Reserve will cut interest rates in
June despite inflation concerns tied to the war in Iran, according to a
Reuters poll.
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoKCigWgeBWcNVoPJ?format=multipart>(3/12)

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

A strategy to manage capital gains taxation <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJoLCigWgeBWcNhhcm?format=multipart>

Reinvesting in a Qualified Opportunity Fund is one way for investors to
manage the capital gains impact of selling an appreciated asset, writes
Michael Kelley of Park View Investments. "The gain is deferred and
unrecognized until the QOF investment is sold or the statutory deferral
period ends," Kelley writes.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoLCigWgeBWcNhhcm?format=multipart>(3/12)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJpeCigWgeBWcNyYyx>

 

Consider spousal IRAs for tax, retirement savings <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRlyDffJfaDHoJoOCigWgeBWcNhCKa?format=multipart>

There may still be time to manage 2025 tax obligations and bolster
retirement savings through the use of spousal IRAs. This strategy is
available to married couples with a single income.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRlyDffJfaDHoJoOCigWgeBWcNhCKa?format=multipart>(3/12)

Top Stories

 

Labor Dept. looks to vacate Biden-era fiduciary rule <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRfQDffJfaDHozqHCigWgeBWcNzvtz?format=multipart>

The Labor Department is backing an effort to vacate the Retirement
Security Rule, which sought to expand fiduciary duty. The rule was
blocked from taking effect after federal courts in Texas issued
nationwide stays in 2024, following legal challenges from Finseca and
other groups.

Brokerage and Industry News

 

Wink: RILA sales show strength in Q4 <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRfQDffJfaDHozqLCigWgeBWcNQgWD?format=multipart>

Total annuity sales reached $116.9 billion in the fourth quarter of
2025, up 16.7% from the same period a year earlier, according to Wink.
Variable annuity sales gained 5.3% from a year ago, and registered
index-linked annuity sales rose 23.8% from the prior year.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRfQDffJfaDHozqLCigWgeBWcNQgWD?format=multipart>(3/11)

Investment News

 

Consumer price index gains 0.3% in Feb. from prior month <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRfQDffJfaDHozqMCigWgeBWcNXPCG?format=multipart>

The consumer price index gained 0.3% on a seasonally adjusted basis in
February, and it rose 2.4% from the prior year, according to the Labor
Department. However, the data came before a surge in oil prices tied to
conflict in the Middle East.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRfQDffJfaDHozqMCigWgeBWcNXPCG?format=multipart>(3/11), Reuters

from the prior year. However, future deficit reports will likely be
affected by the Supreme Court's recent decision affecting US tariffs.

uRfQDffJfaDHozqTCigWgeBWcNAfES?format=multipart>(3/11)

 

 

 
 

Advanced Markets

 

How family boards help preserve a legacy <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRfQDffJfaDHozqUCigWgeBWcNCkmT?format=multipart>

Rather than trying to use rigid legal arrangements to dictate how
subsequent generations use assets, some clients are opting to establish
so-called family boards to create a process of "shared governance." "The
ultimate goal of a Family Board isn't simply to grow the balance sheet,
but to preserve the family itself," writes John Knowlton of Credent
Wealth Management.
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRfQDffJfaDHozqUCigWgeBWcNCkmT?format=multipart>(3/11)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRfQDffJfaDHozrjCigWgeBWcNSVPX>

 

 

 
 

Policy & Regulation

 

Some lawmakers seeking tax cuts affecting millions of Americans
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRfQDffJfaDHozqVCigWgeBWcNFJtG?
format=multipart>

Some Democratic lawmakers are considering proposals that could eliminate
federal income taxes for millions of people. Sen. Chris Van Hollen of
Maryland is planning a proposal to end income taxes for individuals
making under $46,000, and Sen. Cory Booker of New Jersey is seeking a
major increase in the standard deduction. Both proposals would be paired
with other tax increases to mitigate the impact on the deficit.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRfQDffJfaDHozqVCigWgeBWcNFJtG?format=multipart>(3/11)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRfQDffJfaDHozrkCigWgeBWcNaEwa>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Personal loans can lead to debt challenges <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uRfQDffJfaDHozqWCigWgeBWcNKDch?format=multipart>

Americans are increasingly turning to personal loans and balance
transfers to manage record-high credit card debt, which reached $1.28
trillion at the end of 2025. While personal loans may be an appealing
way to consolidate debt, experts note the strategy is unlikely to
succeed unless it is paired with changes in spending habits.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRfQDffJfaDHozqWCigWgeBWcNKDch?format=multipart>(3/11)

Top Stories

 

Research: Why retirees may spend less than they could <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRadDffJfaDHortLCigWgeBWcNWgJn?format=multipart>

Research suggests that retirees may spend less than they are able to in
certain circumstances, and fear of running out of money is not the only
reason. Instead, some retirees simply choose simple strategies for
withdrawals and see no reason to deviate from them.
*Full Story: *PlanAdviser <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRadDffJfaDHortLCigWgeBWcNWgJn?format=multipart>(3/10)

Investment News

 

BofA: Markets may be misreading Fed reaction to oil prices <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRadDffJfaDHortMCigWgeBWcNdPpq?format=multipart>

Investors expecting the Federal Reserve to respond hawkishly to changes
in oil prices may be misreading the central bank's likely reaction, Bank
of America said. The bank noted that energy shocks can also lead to
steady rates or deeper cuts as policymakers balance inflation risks
against a weakening labor market.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRadDffJfaDHortMCigWgeBWcNdPpq?format=multipart>(3/10)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRadDffJfaDHoruhCigWgeBWcNQCJL>

 

Small-business optimism index down in February <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRadDffJfaDHortNCigWgeBWcNmSEb?format=multipart>

The NFIB Small Business Optimism Index declined to 98.8 in February but
remained above the historical average. Actual price increases fell but
remained above the long-term average, highlighting continued
inflationary price pressures.
*Full Story: *Advisor Perspectives <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRadDffJfaDHortNCigWgeBWcNmSEb?format=multipart>(3/10)

Policy & Regulation

 

SEC, CFTC to work together on enforcement <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uRadDffJfaDHortVCigWgeBWcNNdCY?format=multipart>

The Securities and Exchange Commission and the Commodity Futures Trading
Commission intend to work together on examinations for companies that
they both oversee. "Fragmented, redundant enforcement does not increase
deterrence -- it only increases confusion," said SEC Chair Paul Atkins.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRadDffJfaDHortVCigWgeBWcNNdCY?format=multipart>(3/10)

Coping with identity theft during tax season <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uRadDffJfaDHortWCigWgeBWcNRXlz?format=multipart>

Employment-related identity theft is rising, with 31,450 cases reported
to the Federal Trade Commission in the first three quarters of last
year, a 61% increase from 2021. Here is a closer look at the problem and
what to do about it.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRadDffJfaDHortWCigWgeBWcNRXlz?format=multipart>(3/10)

How much should you have saved for retirement by age 65? <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uRadDffJfaDHortXCigWgeBWcNYlro?format=multipart>

A guide from JPMorgan offers benchmarks for retirement savings at ages
50, 55, 60 and 65 based on income, with recommendations that include
maximizing contributions, managing debt and planning for long-term care
and Medicare.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uRadDffJfaDHortXCigWgeBWcNYlro?format=multipart>(3/10)

Top Stories

 

Millennials seek financial advice as wealth transfer looms <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQvEDffJfaDHohBhCigWgeBWcNSsPo?format=multipart>

Millennials are poised to inherit substantial wealth from baby boomer
parents, according to a study by Equitable. Nearly 70% of millennials
expect to receive assets such as cash, real estate and investments. The
study highlights the importance of financial advisors, with 68% of
millennials preferring personalized guidance.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQvEDffJfaDHohBhCigWgeBWcNSsPo?format=multipart>(3/9)

Brokerage and Industry News

 

Study: Some young investors try prediction markets, betting <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQvEDffJfaDHohBiCigWgeBWcNcQUn?format=multipart>

Some young investors who do not feel that they are on track financially
are experimenting with prediction markets, sports betting and
speculative assets, according to a study by Northwestern Mutual. About
32% of Generation Z and 24% of millennials who feel financially behind
are considering or already participating in prediction markets or sports
betting.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews (tiered subscription model) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQvEDffJfaDHohBiCigWgeBWcNcQUn?
format=multipart>(3/9), Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uQvEDffJfaDHohBjCigWgeBWcNoJgQ?format=multipart>(3/9)

Retirement savings snag may arise when workers change jobs <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQvEDffJfaDHoiaaCigWgeBWcNgiNe?format=multipart>

In certain cases, when people change jobs, funds get rolled into Roth
IRAs, and these accounts cannot subsequently be rolled into a 401(k) at
the new employer.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQvEDffJfaDHoiaaCigWgeBWcNgiNe?format=multipart>(3/9)

Survey tracks inflation expectations <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQvEDffJfaDHoiabCigWgeBWcNgSYx?format=multipart>

A survey conducted in February found that Americans' inflation
expectations have generally held steady, with consumers foreseeing
inflation at 3% over the next year. The survey was conducted before a
recent spike in energy prices. Consumers also indicated that they are
less likely to voluntarily quit their jobs.
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQvEDffJfaDHoiabCigWgeBWcNgSYx?format=multipart>(3/9), Bloomberg
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQvEDffJfaDHoiacCigWgeBWcNiXGy?

Industrial Average erasing steep losses to close in the black for the
day. The S&P 500 also rose, gaining 0.8%.

Policy & Regulation

 

Sen. Booker seeks to raise standard deduction <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uQvEDffJfaDHoiagCigWgeBWcNFnhE?format=multipart>

Sen. Cory Booker of New Jersey is set to introduce a bill to expand the
standard deduction to $75,000 for married couples and $37,500 for
individuals. The proposal would also increase the child tax credit and
introduce a $2,400 baby bonus. Booker aims to fund the plan by raising
taxes on high earners and corporations.
*Full Story: *NBC News <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQvEDffJfaDHoiagCigWgeBWcNFnhE?format=multipart>(3/9), Financial Advisor
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQvEDffJfaDHoiahCigWgeBWcNOqwp?

Best Practices

 

Talking to clients about the implications of Trump accounts <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQvEDffJfaDHoiaiCigWgeBWcNYOBo?format=multipart>

The introduction of Trump accounts highlights the importance of starting
the savings process early on, allowing for the benefit of time and
accumulation. Steve Azoury of Azoury Financial notes that "the more
important conversation we should have with our clients is the importance
of developing a savings plan early enough to maximize the benefits of
compounding."
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQvEDffJfaDHoiaiCigWgeBWcNYOBo?format=multipart>(3/9)

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Addressing specific financial challenges facing women savers <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQvEDffJfaDHoiajCigWgeBWcNkGNR?format=multipart>

Women may face certain specific retirement challenges, including the
fact that their longer life expectancies and higher average health care
costs may put additional pressure on financial resources. However,
careful budgeting and working with a financial professional can address
these issues.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQvEDffJfaDHoiajCigWgeBWcNkGNR?format=multipart>(3/9)

Finseca News

 

ICYMI: Monday Brief with Bonnie Godsman <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQvEDffJfaDHoiamCigWgeBWcNgSYx?format=multipart>

Strong professions are strengthened by leaders who invest in people and
expand what leadership can achieve. This week, Bonnie Godsman, President
of GAMA Global, joins Marc Cadin to discuss mentorship, global
leadership, and why advancing women in leadership is essential to the
future of financial services. As we recognize Women's History Month,
their conversation highlights how strong leadership strengthens our
profession and expands access to financial security. Watch the full
Monday Brief in your inbox or here. <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQvEDffJfaDHoiamCigWgeBWcNgSYx>

of GAMA Global, as a 2026 Women of Influence. Godsman has spent her

families achieve financial security," Godsman said.

Policy & Regulation

 

ICYMI: IRS issues proposals for Trump accounts <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQrTDffJfaDHoagKCigWgeBWcNdtia?format=multipart>

The IRS proposed rules for the Trump accounts created by the tax and
spending law passed last summer, including for the $1,000 seed payments
available for some families. Under the proposals, parents would have up
to 17 years to claim these payments.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQrTDffJfaDHoagKCigWgeBWcNdtia?format=multipart>(3/6), The Hill
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQrTDffJfaDHoagLCigWgeBWcNedtt?

uQrTDffJfaDHoagMCigWgeBWcNgibu?format=multipart>(3/6)

Bowman, Miran discuss weak jobs report <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQrTDffJfaDHoagNCigWgeBWcNjHih?format=multipart>

Federal Reserve Vice Chair for Supervision Michelle Bowman and Governor
Stephen Miran have both highlighted the potential case for rate cuts
following the latest US jobs data. The report "confirms to me that the
labor market continues to be weak, and it could use some support from
our policy rate," Bowman said, with Miran noting that "the labor market
can use more accommodation from monetary policy."
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQrTDffJfaDHoagNCigWgeBWcNjHih?format=multipart>(3/6), Bloomberg
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQrTDffJfaDHoagOCigWgeBWcNoAQI?
format=multipart>(3/6)

Balancing retirement savings, college prep <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uQrTDffJfaDHoagPCigWgeBWcNuOWx?format=multipart>

A couple, both 54, face a financial dilemma: One wants to start a
college fund for their grandson, but the other wants to focus on
bolstering their $1.8 million retirement savings. Financial pros
emphasize that financial security should take priority and emphasize the
value of choosing educational accounts strategically. Options include a
529 account or a brokerage account focused on college savings.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQrTDffJfaDHoagPCigWgeBWcNuOWx?format=multipart>(3/8)

Optimize estate planning with effective family meetings <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQlvDffJfaDHnrgpCigWgeBWcNDTmw?format=multipart>

Family meetings are crucial for effective estate planning and long-term
wealth management, notes Michael Schneider of The Lerner Group.
Schneider recommends asking five questions to guide these meetings,
starting with determining who should attend, what should be discussed
and where and when the meeting should take place.

Labor productivity rose more than expected in the fourth quarter,
climbing at a 2.8% annualized rate, according to Bureau of Labor

Policy & Regulation

 

Fed's Barkin on what Middle East conflict means for monetary policy
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQlvDffJfaDHnrgACigWgeBWcNFnOk?
format=multipart>

Richmond Federal Reserve President Tom Barkin said the central bank's
response to conflict in the Middle East will depend on the duration of
the impact on the US economy. "Textbook monetary policy would be you
look through a short-term shock, but you don't look through a long-term
shock, and I think that's a lot of the assessment people are going to
have to make," Barkin said.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQlvDffJfaDHnrgACigWgeBWcNFnOk?format=multipart>(3/5), Reuters <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQlvDffJfaDHnrgBCigWgeBWcNIMUX?format=multipart>(3/5)

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Avoid emotional reactions when the market dips <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQlvDffJfaDHnrgDCigWgeBWcNTUJn?format=multipart>

Significant dips in the stock market may occur more often than average
investors realize, with a Morningstar Direct analysis finding that the
S&P 500 has fallen by 1% or more on 1,001 days during the past three
decades. "We believe investors need to try and keep a clear head, look
through the headlines, and stick to a well thought out plan," Scott Wren
of the Wells Fargo Investment Institute wrote in a recent market commentary.
*Full Story: *CNBC <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQlvDffJfaDHnrgDCigWgeBWcNTUJn?format=multipart>(3/5)

resp/uQlvDffJfaDHnrgECigWgeBWcNbDpq> -- a global recognition honoring

including her work advancing career leadership and strengthening the
global community through GAMA Global.

401(k) balances rise, but so do hardship withdrawals <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoGCigWgeBWcNboUN?format=multipart>

The average 401(k) balance rose to $146,400 in 2025, up by $14,700,
according to Fidelity Investments, and the average IRA balance also
climbed. However, Vanguard research shows that more Americans are
digging into their retirement accounts, with 6% of 401(k) participants
taking hardship withdrawals last year.

LIMRA: New individual life premium hit record in 2025 <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoJCigWgeBWcNyoVq?format=multipart>

Total new individual life insurance premium rose to $17.5 billion last
year, according to LIMRA, up 10% from the prior year. Growth is expected
to continue in 2026, although at a somewhat slower pace, according to
LIMRA's Sean Grindall.

Investment News

 

Bessent says global tariff will rise to 15% this week <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoKCigWgeBWcNINap?format=multipart>

Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent said President Donald Trump's global
tariff will increase from 10% to 15% this week. This change follows a
Supreme Court ruling that invalidated Trump's previous unilateral tariff
hikes. Bessent predicts that the tariffs will revert to their former
levels by August, as the current tariffs, imposed under Section 122 of
the Trade Act of 1974, can only last 150 days without Congressional
approval. During this period, the US Trade Representative and Commerce
Department will conduct studies to potentially impose further tariffs.
*Full Story: *Quartz <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQfJDffJfaDHnjoKCigWgeBWcNINap?format=multipart>(3/4), CNBC <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoLCigWgeBWcNUFmS?
format=multipart>(3/4), The New York Times <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoOCigWgeBWcNVaUG?format=multipart>(3/4)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjpiCigWgeBWcNTUOF>

 

Fed report: Economy seeing slight to moderate gains <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoPCigWgeBWcNVLfZ?format=multipart>

The US economy saw slight to moderate gains in activity across most
regions in recent weeks, according to the Federal Reserve's latest Beige
Book survey. However, "sales were dampened by economic uncertainty,
increased price sensitivity and lower-income consumers pulling back on
spending," the report notes.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQfJDffJfaDHnjoPCigWgeBWcNVLfZ?format=multipart>(3/4), The Wall Street
Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoQCigWgeBWcNXPOa?
format=multipart>(3/4)

Advanced Markets

 

Planning considerations for high-net-worth families <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoUCigWgeBWcNufpg?format=multipart>

Specificity is key for estate planning success with the highest-net-
worth families, writes Patrick Schultz of Clark Capital Management. "For
clients with potential estate tax issues, a truly robust financial plan
should identify available excess capital that can be strategically
deployed into irrevocable structures, such as Spousal Lifetime Access
Trusts (SLATs) or Gift Trusts, designed to preserve wealth in a tax-
efficient manner well into the future," Schultz writes.
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQfJDffJfaDHnjoUCigWgeBWcNufpg?format=multipart>(3/4)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjpmCigWgeBWcNiXGy>

 

 

 
 

Policy & Regulation

 

Fed's Miran voices continued support for rate cuts <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoVCigWgeBWcNDiDR?format=multipart>

Federal Reserve Governor Stephen Miran says the recent conflict in the
Middle East has not affected his economic forecasts, and he still
supports interest-rate cuts. "When you look at the totality of labor-
market data, there's still evidence to me that it needs more support
from monetary policy," Miran says.
*Full Story: *Seeking Alpha <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQfJDffJfaDHnjoVCigWgeBWcNDiDR?format=multipart>(3/4), Bloomberg
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjoWCigWgeBWcNNGIQ?
format=multipart>(3/4)

Best Practices

 

Family offices and succession planning <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQfJDffJfaDHnjoXCigWgeBWcNZyVt?format=multipart>

Family offices are facing a critical inflection point as leadership
transitions accelerate over the next decade, raising structural, legal
and operational challenges. "An office ready for transition maintains
current, executed versions of operating agreements; trust instruments;
shareholder/member agreements; employment contracts; services
agreements; powers of attorney for health care and property; and
incapacity procedures," write Christopher Roman and Abigail Scott Myers
of ArentFox Schiff.
*Full Story: *WealthManagement <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQfJDffJfaDHnjoXCigWgeBWcNZyVt?format=multipart>(3/4)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjpoCigWgeBWcNlMzS>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Giving inheritance early: Pros, cons for retirees <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uQfJDffJfaDHnjpaCigWgeBWcNbpav?format=multipart>

Retirees considering giving their children an advance on their
inheritance should ensure fairness and proper documentation, says
Patrick Simasko of Simasko Law. "Financially assisting them with a first
home purchase or daycare costs can have a greater impact on their lives
now than an inheritance might decades from now," Simasko says.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uQfJDffJfaDHnjpaCigWgeBWcNbpav?format=multipart>(3/4)

Research: Couples who coordinate finances may save more <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPATDffJfaDHmZxyCigWgeBWcNQoJr?format=multipart>

Couples could be leaving substantial retirement money on the table by
not coordinating their 401(k) contributions, according to research
appearing in American Economic Review. The study found that couples who
fail to allocate savings to the account with the highest employer match
could miss out on an average of $14,000 in retirement wealth over their
lifetime.

Strategies for the Great Wealth Transfer <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPATDffJfaDHmZxKCigWgeBWcNXXpu?format=multipart>

Tens of trillions of dollars are expected to transfer from one
generation to the next in the coming decades, posing challenges for
families and creating opportunities for advisors to add value. Family
governance, heir education and tax planning are crucial for successful
wealth transfer, writes Assunta "Susie" McLane of Summit Place.

Retirement bill seeks to protect domestic violence survivors <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPATDffJfaDHmZxLCigWgeBWcNjPBX?format=multipart>

Recently introduced legislation would adjust notification and consent
requirements for retirement benefits in certain cases in a move designed
to protect survivors of domestic violence. The bill "is a common-sense
and critical reform that protects survivors of domestic violence,
ensuring they can maintain their privacy from those who have caused them
immense and unimaginable harm," said Rep. Joe Neguse of Colorado.
*Full Story: *ThinkAdvisor (free registration) <https://

Annuities can be a valuable tool for clients considering early

implications and ensure a steady income.

ICYMI: How annuities help clients manage market risk <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPvsDffJfaDHmQjMCigWgeBWcNpzkw?format=multipart>

Annuities can help clients hedge against market risk while offering
options for accumulation. "With a fixed index annuity (FIA), clients get
benefits similar to fixed annuities but instead of interest driving
accumulation potential it's index-linked credits," writes Dave Byrnes of
Security Benefit. "Clients are not actually invested in the financial
indices, so they don't experience loss in the contract due to market
downturns."

What the Great Wealth Transfer could mean for women <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPvsDffJfaDHmQjQCigWgeBWcNLOLC?format=multipart>

The "Great Wealth Transfer" could see an estimated $105 trillion passed
to heirs by 2048, with $54 trillion going to spouses, according to
Cerulli Associates. Women could inherit a large portion of this sum,
given their longer life expectancies.

Entrepreneurs can prepare for a business exit from the start, with
financial planner Jamie Carroll advising business owners to maintain
transparent financials, align legal structures with personal goals,
scale the business independently of the founder, protect intellectual
property, and plan for long-term objectives.

Retirement savings tips for a young adult <https://r.smartbrief.com/
resp/uPvsDffJfaDHmQjSCigWgeBWcNfqfm?format=multipart>

Kiplinger's David Crook shares investing advice for young adults,
emphasizing goal setting, continuous investing and balancing risk.

Finseca News

 

Just Announced: John Boehner & Kyrsten Sinema at Advanced Markets
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPvsDffJfaDHmQjTCigWgeBWcNrirP?
format=multipart>

At Advanced Markets, hear directly from former Speaker John Boehner and
former U.S. Senator Kyrsten Sinema — two leaders who went head-to-head
with their own parties and understand what happens when politics turns
inward. Get insight into what gridlock means for tax, retirement, and
regulatory policy — and the context you need when clients ask, "What's
next?" Book your ticket today. <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPvsDffJfaDHmQjTCigWgeBWcNrirP>

ICYMI: Monday Brief with AmeriLife Wealth's Todd Buchanan <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPvsDffJfaDHmQjWCigWgeBWcNvNFN?format=multipart>

Strong professions are built by leaders who invest in the future — not
just their own success. This week, Todd Buchanan, President of AmeriLife
Wealth and a Finseca Board member, shares why firms must be structured
to meet the full needs of families as regulations evolve and advice
continues to change. More than 13,000 advisors now stand together
through Finseca to protect access to advice and strengthen our
collective voice — watch the full Monday Brief in your inbox or watch
here. <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPvsDffJfaDHmQjWCigWgeBWcNvNFN>

Top Stories

 

Advocacy is key with midterm elections on the horizon <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdvCigWgeBWcNUFwg?format=multipart>

Advocacy will be crucial in 2026 as midterm elections approach, writes
Finseca's Jennifer Fox, who discusses key legislative developments in
2025 and what to expect going forward. "Election cycles create critical
opportunities to educate candidates and incumbents about the role
financial professionals play in helping families, small businesses and
communities achieve long-term security," Fox writes.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPrbDffJfaDHmKdvCigWgeBWcNUFwg?format=multipart>(3/1)

Brokerage and Industry News

 

Annuity market thrives on innovation, demographic shifts <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdyCigWgeBWcNTGCg?format=multipart>

The US annuity market grew in 2025, with sales reaching $461.3 billion,
according to LIMRA. Growth is driven in part by registered index-linked
annuities, which benefit from "economic and market uncertainty,
demographic shifts, and product innovation," writes Corey Walther of
Allianz Financial Services.
*Full Story: *Advisor Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPrbDffJfaDHmKdyCigWgeBWcNTGCg?format=multipart>(3/1)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdOCigWgeBWcNjHTp>

 

 

 
 

Investment News

 

US job growth expected to slow after strong January <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdzCigWgeBWcNUqNz?format=multipart>

US job growth for February is expected to have slowed compared with
January, with economists surveyed by Bloomberg forecasting the addition
of 60,000 jobs. The unemployment rate for February is projected to
remain at 4.3%.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPrbDffJfaDHmKdzCigWgeBWcNUqNz?format=multipart>(3/1)

Policy & Regulation

 

ICYMI: Proposals to tax high earners spread beyond California <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdCCigWgeBWcNeOkO?format=multipart>

An effort is underway in California to tax billionaires' personal
wealth, although the proposal faces opposition from Gov. Gavin Newsom
and others. Meanwhile, other states are considering their own proposals
to raise levies on high earners. In Washington state, for example,
lawmakers are weighing a 9.9% tax on incomes above $1 million.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPrbDffJfaDHmKdCCigWgeBWcNeOkO?format=multipart>(2/27)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdQCigWgeBWcNxEFh>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Tips for avoiding RMD mistakes <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPrbDffJfaDHmKdDCigWgeBWcNlcqD?format=multipart>

About 7% of people required to take RMDs take less than the required
amount, according to Vanguard, which can result in a penalty. Financial
planner Evan Beach advises learning the rules, consolidating accounts,
automating distributions and seeking forgiveness from the IRS if a
distribution is missed.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPrbDffJfaDHmKdDCigWgeBWcNlcqD?format=multipart>(3/1)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdRCigWgeBWcNGHTS>

 

Managing the transition from saving to spending in retirement <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdECigWgeBWcNsKWG?format=multipart>

Making the transition from saving to spending can be difficult for new
retirees, even those who have saved diligently for decades. One way to
address this problem is to create a "retirement paycheck" by automating
transfers from investment accounts to checking accounts at the same time
each month.
*Full Story: *The New York Times <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPrbDffJfaDHmKdECigWgeBWcNsKWG?format=multipart>(2/28)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdSCigWgeBWcNRfYR>

 

How to manage the first month after a relative's death <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPrbDffJfaDHmKdFCigWgeBWcNBOlr?format=multipart>

The first month after a family member's death is a challenging period
marked by emotional distress and administrative demands, writes Howard
Enders of The Estate Registry. Enders emphasizes the importance of
preparation, including digital estate planning and clear communication
roles, to ease this transition.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPrbDffJfaDHmKdFCigWgeBWcNBOlr?format=multipart>(3/1)

Top Stories

 

Finseca surpasses 13K members, showing momentum for financial security
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmzBcCigWgeBWcNVZEK?
format=multipart>

Finseca now represents more than 13,000 members across the country,
highlighting momentum in the financial security profession. A key part
of that growth involves Northwestern Mutual, which has exceeded 2,000
members within Finseca, becoming the second entity to reach that
milestone after M Financial. "Protecting access to financial advice
requires scale, unity, and action -- milestones like this move us
forward," said Finseca CEO Marc Cadin.
*Full Story: *Yahoo <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPlkDffJfaDHmzBcCigWgeBWcNVZEK?format=multipart>(2/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAapCigWgeBWcNTrDr>

 

 

 
 

Brokerage and Industry News

 

Research: Millennials entering "peak 35" <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPlkDffJfaDHmzBdCigWgeBWcNZyLx?format=multipart>

Many millennials are turning 35 this year, representing their entry into
their peak earnings, investing and planning years. At the same time,
some stand to inherit wealth from aging relatives. "This historic
transfer of wealth is about more than passing down assets," says Gerald
Grant of Equitable Advisors. "It's about passing down values."
*Full Story: *Financial Advisor <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPlkDffJfaDHmzBdCigWgeBWcNZyLx?format=multipart>(2/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAaqCigWgeBWcNYllS>

 

Study shows growing mortality from severe heart attacks among younger
Americans <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmzBeCigWgeBWcNestY?
format=multipart>

A growing share of adults between the ages of 18 and 54 are dying of
severe first heart attacks, according to the American Heart Association,
potentially a sign of worsening health among younger adults.
*Full Story: *The Wall Street Journal <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPlkDffJfaDHmzBeCigWgeBWcNestY?format=multipart>(2/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAarCigWgeBWcNezrH>

 

 

 
 

Investment News

 

US, China jockey over tariff policy <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPlkDffJfaDHmzBfCigWgeBWcNkGzN?format=multipart>

The US is looking to maintain relatively high tariff rates on China
after the Supreme Court's decision created uncertainty about trade
policy. President Donald Trump is set to travel to Beijing in late March
to discuss an extension of a tariff truce with China's Xi Jinping.
*Full Story: *Bloomberg <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPlkDffJfaDHmzBfCigWgeBWcNkGzN?format=multipart>(2/25)

Policy & Regulation

 

Labor Dept. releases independent contractor rule <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAaaCigWgeBWcNMtrR?format=multipart>

The Labor Department released a new rule related to the classification
of workers as independent contractors, and the department says it will
accept comments until April 28. The proposal "would rescind the
department's 2024 final rule addressing the classification of
independent contractors and replace it with an analysis for employee
classification similar to the one adopted by the department in 2021,"
according to a statement.
*Full Story: *ThinkAdvisor (free registration) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAaaCigWgeBWcNMtrR?
format=multipart>(2/26), InvestmentNews (tiered subscription model)
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAabCigWgeBWcNNdDk?

uPlkDffJfaDHmAacCigWgeBWcNPill?format=multipart>(2/26), Reuters
<https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAadCigWgeBWcNSHrY?
format=multipart>(2/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAatCigWgeBWcNvlmv>

 

Fed's Miran sees 4 rate cuts in 2026 amid labor market risks <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAaeCigWgeBWcNXBaz?format=multipart>

Federal Reserve Board Governor Stephen Miran supports four quarter-point
rate cuts this year, citing ongoing labor market risks. "I definitely
think the labor market can be supported by the Federal Reserve further,"
Miran says. "I really do not think that we have an inflation problem."
*Full Story: *Reuters <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPlkDffJfaDHmAaeCigWgeBWcNXBaz?format=multipart>(2/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAauCigWgeBWcNFJru>

 

 

 
 

Best Practices

 

A successful business sale takes proactive planning <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAafCigWgeBWcNdPgo?format=multipart>

Spring is an ideal time to sell businesses, but a successful transaction
may take years of planning, creating an example for financial
professionals to add value. "I think about business and exit planning as
a continuous cycle rather than a linear process," says Amy Knox of
Axtella. "Owners who have never sold a business can find tremendous
value in working with an advisor who can help them get started and guide
them through what can often be a very involved process."
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews (tiered subscription model) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAafCigWgeBWcNdPgo?format=multipart>(2/26)

share-text <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAavCigWgeBWcNRBDX>

 

 

 
 

To Share with Your Clients or COIs

 

Why retirees may need to reevaluate investment strategy <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPlkDffJfaDHmAagCigWgeBWcNlxMr?format=multipart>

The "buy and hold" investment strategy may need to be tweaked for
retirees, who cannot offset temporary losses with a paycheck, writes
Steven Kao of Kirsner Wealth Management. "'Buy and hold' may sound
reassuring, but for those who cannot easily replace lost capital, it is
often a passive and risky approach," he writes.
*Full Story: *Kiplinger <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPlkDffJfaDHmAagCigWgeBWcNlxMr?format=multipart>(2/26)

Top Stories

 

Cadin: Trump's message shows financial security "does not happen by
chance" <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPfaDffJfaDHmqlDCigWgeBWcNpmlF?
format=multipart>

During his State of the Union address, President Donald Trump called for
offering retirement plans to workers who don't have access to 401(k)s.
In addition, he called for matching contributions up to $1,000 annually.
"The President's message reinforces a simple truth: financial security
does not happen by chance -- it requires a plan," said Finseca CEO Marc
Cadin. "Finseca looks forward to working with the administration to
expand access to financial planning and help more American families gain
peace of mind."
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPfaDffJfaDHmqlDCigWgeBWcNpmlF?format=multipart>(2/25)

r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPfaDffJfaDHmqlECigWgeBWcNrqTG?format=multipart>

tariff to 15% "where appropriate," while maintaining tariffs on Chinese
goods between 35% and 50%, according to U.S. Trade Representative
Jamieson Greer.

Best Practices

 

Regular reviews key to ensuring annuity effectiveness <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPfaDffJfaDHmqlJCigWgeBWcNWJJx?format=multipart>

Annual annuity reviews are crucial for ensuring that financial products
are continuing to serve clients' evolving needs, says Jason Bernat, CEO
of American Financial Services. Advisors should use clear language,
focus on client goals, and document discussions to foster strong
relationships.
*Full Story: *InsuranceNewsNet Magazine <https://r.smartbrief.com/resp/
uPfaDffJfaDHmqlJCigWgeBWcNWJJx?format=multipart>(2/25)

Generative AI seen as having major impact on some practices <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPfaDffJfaDHmqlKCigWgeBWcNhhOw?format=multipart>

Generative AI is helping shape the future of wealth management, with 28%
of respondents to a Broadridge Financial Solutions survey saying it is
having the greatest impact on their business. "The acceleration of
generative AI is foundational to how firms operate, compete, and serve
clients," says Germán Soto Sanchez of Broadridge.
*Full Story: *InvestmentNews (tiered subscription model) <https://
r.smartbrief.com/resp/uPfaDffJfaDHmqlKCigWgeBWcNhhOw?format=multipart>(2/25)

Small-business owners may want to consider retirement plans such as solo
401(k)s, which allow contributions as both an employer and employee.

surrounding loans from them.
